# The following defines the version and build number for your application.
# A version number is three numbers separated by dots, like 1.2.43
# followed by an optional build number separated by a +.
# Both the version and the builder number may be overridden in flutter
# build by specifying --build-name and --build-number, respectively.
# In Android, build-name is used as versionName while build-number used as versionCode.
# Read more about Android versioning at https://developer.android.com/studio/publish/versioning
# In iOS, build-name is used as CFBundleShortVersionString while build-number is used as CFBundleVersion.
# Read more about iOS versioning at
# https://developer.apple.com/library/archive/documentation/General/Reference/InfoPlistKeyReference/Articles/CoreFoundationKeys.html
# In Windows, build-name is used as the major, minor, and patch parts
# of the product and file versions while build-number is used as the build suffix.

flutter_native_splash:
  color: "#FAFAFA"
  # A padded variant of the launcher icon, not the raw icon itself — the
  # raw one has no internal margin, so both the legacy splash and
  # (especially) Android 12+'s own masked/circular splash icon API were
  # rendering it edge-to-edge, reading as oversized/cropped ("زوم"). This
  # one keeps the visible glyph to about 59% of the canvas, matching
  # Android's own guidance for its splash icon safe zone.
  image: assets/icons/icon_splash.png
  android_12:
    color: "#FAFAFA"
    image: assets/icons/icon_splash.png

===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\android\app\src\main\AndroidManifest.xml =====
<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android">
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET"/>
    <!-- In-app update system (core/update/) — downloads a new APK from
         Supabase Storage and hands it to open_filex, which opens
         Android's own package-install screen for it. -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES"/>
    <!-- Notifications (core/notifications/) — line-expiry/debt/balance
         reminders scheduled via flutter_local_notifications, delivered
         through Android's AlarmManager even while the app isn't
         running. POST_NOTIFICATIONS/VIBRATE are merged in automatically
         by the plugin's own manifest; RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED plus the
         plugin's own receivers below let it re-arm any still-pending
         alarms after a device reboot (Android clears AlarmManager
         alarms on reboot otherwise) — on top of this app's own
         reschedule-from-Drift pass on every cold start
         (NotificationsCubit._syncThenLoad), which is still the primary
         mechanism since a reboot's receiver alone can't recover an
         alarm for something created/changed after the last time the
         plugin's native side saved it. -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED"/>
    <!-- Support & Feedback: attach a photo (image_picker) and record a
         short voice note (record). -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.CAMERA"/>
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO"/>
    <uses-feature android:name="android.hardware.camera" android:required="false"/>
    <uses-feature android:name="android.hardware.camera.autofocus" android:required="false"/>
    <uses-feature android:name="android.hardware.microphone" android:required="false"/>
    <application
        android:label="eSIM Dafter"
        android:name="${applicationName}"
        android:icon="@mipmap/ic_launcher">
        <activity
            android:name=".MainActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:launchMode="singleTop"
            android:taskAffinity=""
            android:theme="@style/LaunchTheme"
            android:configChanges="orientation|keyboardHidden|keyboard|screenSize|smallestScreenSize|locale|layoutDirection|fontScale|screenLayout|density|uiMode"
            android:hardwareAccelerated="true"
            android:windowSoftInputMode="adjustResize">
            <!-- Specifies an Android theme to apply to this Activity as soon as
                 the Android process has started. This theme is visible to the user
                 while the Flutter UI initializes. After that, this theme continues
                 to determine the Window background behind the Flutter UI. -->
            <meta-data
              android:name="io.flutter.embedding.android.NormalTheme"
              android:resource="@style/NormalTheme"
              />
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN"/>
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER"/>
            </intent-filter>
            <!-- Supabase Auth "reset password" email link deep link — see
                 core/config/deep_links.dart. autoVerify is left off since
                 this is a custom scheme, not an https App Link. -->
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.VIEW"/>
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.DEFAULT"/>
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.BROWSABLE"/>
                <data android:scheme="simsalesmanager" android:host="reset-callback"/>
            </intent-filter>
        </activity>
        <!-- Don't delete the meta-data below.
             This is used by the Flutter tool to generate GeneratedPluginRegistrant.java -->
        <meta-data
            android:name="flutterEmbedding"
            android:value="2" />
        <receiver android:exported="false" android:name="com.dexterous.flutterlocalnotifications.ActionBroadcastReceiver" />
        <receiver android:exported="false" android:name="com.dexterous.flutterlocalnotifications.ScheduledNotificationReceiver" />
        <receiver android:exported="false" android:name="com.dexterous.flutterlocalnotifications.ScheduledNotificationBootReceiver">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.BOOT_COMPLETED"/>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MY_PACKAGE_REPLACED"/>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.QUICKBOOT_POWERON" />
                <action android:name="com.htc.intent.action.QUICKBOOT_POWERON"/>
            </intent-filter>
        </receiver>
    </application>
    <!-- Required to query activities that can process text, see:
         https://developer.android.com/training/package-visibility and
         https://developer.android.com/reference/android/content/Intent#ACTION_PROCESS_TEXT.

         In particular, this is used by the Flutter engine in io.flutter.plugin.text.ProcessTextPlugin. -->
    <queries>
        <intent>
            <action android:name="android.intent.action.PROCESS_TEXT"/>
            <data android:mimeType="text/plain"/>
        </intent>
    </queries>
</manifest>

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:drift/drift.dart' show Value;
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'core/auth/auth_service.dart';
import 'core/config/env.dart';
import 'core/database/app_database.dart';
import 'core/di/service_locator.dart';

import 'core/navigation/route_names.dart';
import 'core/sync/bloc/sync_bloc.dart';
import 'core/sync/bloc/sync_event.dart';
import 'core/sync/connectivity_service.dart';
import 'core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'core/theme/cubit/theme_cubit.dart';
import 'features/activity_log/presentation/pages/activity_log_page.dart';
import 'features/admin/presentation/pages/admin_company_catalog_page.dart';
import 'features/admin/presentation/pages/admin_hub_page.dart';
import 'features/admin/presentation/pages/admin_support_feedback_page.dart';
import 'features/admin/presentation/pages/admin_users_page.dart';

import 'features/customers/presentation/pages/customers_page.dart';
import 'features/home/presentation/pages/home_page.dart';
import 'features/more/presentation/pages/more_page.dart';
import 'features/new_sale/presentation/pages/new_sale_sheet.dart';
import 'features/number_update/presentation/pages/number_update_page.dart';
import 'features/notifications/presentation/pages/notifications_page.dart';
import 'features/numbers/presentation/pages/numbers_page.dart';
import 'features/auth/presentation/pages/reset_password_page.dart';
import 'features/profits/presentation/pages/profits_page.dart';
import 'features/reports/presentation/pages/reports_page.dart';
import 'features/settings/presentation/pages/settings_page.dart';
import 'features/shipments_operations/presentation/pages/shipments_operations_page.dart';
import 'features/support_feedback/presentation/pages/support_feedback_page.dart';

void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  await EasyLocalization.ensureInitialized();

  // Only connects when real project credentials are supplied via
  // `--dart-define` (see core/config/env.dart) — otherwise the app runs
  // fully offline against the local Drift database, per CLAUDE.md's
  // offline-first rule.
  var startRoute = RouteNames.login;
  if (Env.isConfigured) {
    await Supabase.initialize(
      url: Env.supabaseUrl,
      publishableKey: Env.supabaseAnonKey,
    );

    // Needed below to read/write the cached role and check real
    // connectivity — safe to set up this early, before the rest of DI,
    // since neither depends on anything else in the container.
    setupServiceLocator();

    // `Supabase.initialize` already restores any previously-persisted
    // session (supabase_flutter stores it locally by default) before
    // this line runs — so a non-null `currentSession` here means the
    // user is genuinely still signed in, not just that a stale session
    // object happens to exist in memory. Skip Login straight to Home in
    // that case, instead of always forcing a fresh sign-in on every app
    // launch regardless of session state.
    final ownerId = Supabase.instance.client.auth.currentSession?.user.id;
    if (ownerId != null) {
      startRoute = RouteNames.home;
      // An admin account never sees the seller shell — same routing
      // decision `LoginCubit.submit()` makes right after a fresh
      // sign-in. Read whatever role was cached from the last successful
      // fetch first (instant, no network) so a returning admin lands on
      // the right shell even fully offline, then only attempt a fresh
      // fetch when there's real connectivity to back it — otherwise
      // this used to block the very first frame on the platform's long
      // default connect timeout for nothing (see ConnectivityService's
      // doc comment on why a plain `connectivity_plus` check isn't
      // enough on its own).
      final db = getIt<AppDatabase>();
      var role = await _readCachedRole(db, ownerId);
      if (await getIt<ConnectivityService>().isOnline) {
        try {
          role = await AuthService(Supabase.instance.client).fetchCurrentRole();
          unawaited(_cacheRole(db, ownerId, role));
        } catch (_) {
          // Falls back to the cached role read above.
        }
      }
      if (role == 'admin') startRoute = RouteNames.adminHub;
    }
  } else {
    setupServiceLocator();
  }

  getIt<SyncBloc>().add(const SyncStarted());
  NewSaleLauncher.register((context) => NewSaleSheet.show(context));
  runApp(SimSalesManagerApp(initialRoute: startRoute));
}

Future<String?> _readCachedRole(AppDatabase db, String ownerId) async {
  try {
    final row = await (db.select(
      db.settingsTable,
    )..where((t) => t.ownerId.equals(ownerId))).getSingleOrNull();
    return row?.cachedRole;
  } catch (_) {
    return null;
  }
}

Future<void> _cacheRole(AppDatabase db, String ownerId, String role) async {
  try {
    await db
        .into(db.settingsTable)
        .insertOnConflictUpdate(
          SettingsTableCompanion.insert(
            ownerId: ownerId,
            updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
            cachedRole: Value(role),
          ),
        );
  } catch (_) {
    // Best-effort cache write — a miss here just means the next offline
    // cold start falls back to Login-decided-nothing (stays on the
    // seller shell), same as before this existed.
  }
}

class SimSalesManagerApp extends StatefulWidget {
  final String initialRoute;

  const SimSalesManagerApp({super.key, this.initialRoute = RouteNames.login});

  @override
  State<SimSalesManagerApp> createState() => _SimSalesManagerAppState();
}

class _SimSalesManagerAppState extends State<SimSalesManagerApp> {
  final _navigatorKey = GlobalKey<NavigatorState>();
  StreamSubscription<AuthState>? _authSubscription;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Supabase's own deep link handling (bundled `app_links`, see
    // `core/config/deep_links.dart`) exchanges the "reset password" email
    // link for a temporary session and fires this event — push the "set
    // new password" screen when it does. Only relevant once a real
    // Supabase project is configured (`main()` only calls
    // `Supabase.initialize` in that case).
    if (Env.isConfigured) {
      _authSubscription = Supabase.instance.client.auth.onAuthStateChange
          .listen((data) {
            if (data.event == AuthChangeEvent.passwordRecovery) {
              _navigatorKey.currentState?.pushNamed(RouteNames.resetPassword);
            }
          });
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _authSubscription?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return EasyLocalization(
      supportedLocales: const [Locale('en'), Locale('ar')],
      path: 'assets/translations',
      fallbackLocale: const Locale('en'),
      startLocale: const Locale('en'),
      child: BlocProvider(
        create: (_) => ThemeCubit(),
        child: BlocBuilder<ThemeCubit, ThemeMode>(
          builder: (context, themeMode) {
            return MaterialApp(
              navigatorKey: _navigatorKey,
              title: 'eSIM Dafter',
              debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
              theme: AppTheme.light,
              darkTheme: AppTheme.dark,
              themeMode: themeMode,
              localizationsDelegates: context.localizationDelegates,
              supportedLocales: context.supportedLocales,
              locale: context.locale,
              initialRoute: widget.initialRoute,
              routes: {
                RouteNames.login: (_) => const LoginPage(),
                RouteNames.resetPassword: (_) => const ResetPasswordPage(),
                RouteNames.home: (_) => const HomePage(),
                RouteNames.companies: (_) => const CompaniesPage(),
                RouteNames.customers: (_) => const CustomersPage(),
                RouteNames.more: (_) => const MorePage(),
                RouteNames.settings: (_) => const SettingsPage(),
                RouteNames.numbers: (_) => const NumbersPage(),
                RouteNames.activityLog: (_) => const ActivityLogPage(),
                RouteNames.supportFeedback: (_) => const SupportFeedbackPage(),
                RouteNames.profits: (_) => const ProfitsPage(),
                RouteNames.reports: (_) => const ReportsPage(),
                RouteNames.shipmentsOperations: (_) =>
                    const ShipmentsOperationsPage(),
                RouteNames.numberUpdate: (_) => const NumberUpdatePage(),
                RouteNames.notifications: (_) => const NotificationsPage(),
                RouteNames.adminHub: (_) => const AdminHubPage(),
                RouteNames.adminCompanyCatalog: (_) =>
                    const AdminCompanyCatalogPage(),
                RouteNames.adminUsers: (_) => const AdminUsersPage(),
                RouteNames.adminSupportFeedback: (_) =>
                    const AdminSupportFeedbackPage(),
              },
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\activity_log\activity_action_type.dart =====
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';

/// Per APP_CONTEXT.md: "single-user, so no User column needed" (per
/// seller — each row is still tenant-scoped by `owner_id`). Colors map
/// onto the app's existing accent palette (no blue, per
/// DESIGN_SYSTEM.md) rather than the reference app's blue/amber/green/red
/// scheme: create/login -> neutral, edit -> warning, delete -> danger,
/// fullCollection -> success.
///
/// Lives in `core/` (not `features/activity_log/`) because every
/// mutating Cubit across the app needs to reference it to log an entry —
/// see `ActivityLogger`.
enum ActivityActionType { login, create, edit, delete, fullCollection }

extension ActivityActionTypeLabel on ActivityActionType {
  String get label {
    switch (this) {
      case ActivityActionType.login:
        return 'activityLog.actionType.login'.tr();
      case ActivityActionType.create:
        return 'activityLog.actionType.create'.tr();
      case ActivityActionType.edit:
        return 'activityLog.actionType.edit'.tr();
      case ActivityActionType.delete:
        return 'activityLog.actionType.delete'.tr();
      case ActivityActionType.fullCollection:
        return 'activityLog.actionType.fullCollection'.tr();
    }
  }
}

String activityActionTypeToRow(ActivityActionType type) => type.name;

ActivityActionType activityActionTypeFromRow(String value) {
  return ActivityActionType.values.firstWhere((t) => t.name == value, orElse: () => ActivityActionType.edit);
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\activity_log\activity_logger.dart =====
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

import '../auth/auth_service.dart';
import '../config/env.dart';
import '../database/app_database.dart';
import '../di/service_locator.dart';
import '../sync/sync_engine.dart';
import '../utils/uuid.dart';
import 'activity_action_type.dart';

/// Cross-cutting audit trail: any mutating Cubit calls
/// `getIt<ActivityLogger>().log(...)` right after a real write succeeds
/// (Companies, Customers, Lines, Shipments, Settings, Profile, Login).
/// A single shared class rather than each Cubit writing to
/// `activity_log` itself, since the insert shape is identical everywhere
/// and this is the one thing every feature needs from `core/` without a
/// cross-feature import.
///
/// No-op in mock/offline mode (`Env.isConfigured` false, or no signed-in
/// owner yet) — there's nothing to log for a demo session, and no
/// `owner_id` to tag a row with anyway. Fire-and-forget like every other
/// write in this app: a failure (offline, transient error) just leaves
/// the row out of Drift; since these are only ever appended (never read
/// back within the same call), there's no `synced = false` row to worry
/// about losing except via the normal sync engine picking it up next
/// time — except here, if the *local* Drift insert itself never
/// happened, there is nothing for sync to find. That's an accepted gap
/// for a nice-to-have audit trail (not a source of truth for balances or
/// anything else the app depends on).
class ActivityLogger {
  final AppDatabase? _db;
  final AuthService? _auth;
  final SyncEngine? _syncEngine;

  ActivityLogger({AppDatabase? db, AuthService? auth, SyncEngine? syncEngine})
    : _db = db ?? (Env.isConfigured ? getIt<AppDatabase>() : null),
      _auth = auth ?? (Env.isConfigured ? getIt<AuthService>() : null),
      _syncEngine = syncEngine ?? (Env.isConfigured ? getIt<SyncEngine>() : null);

  void log(ActivityActionType type, String details) {
    final db = _db;
    final ownerId = _auth?.currentOwnerId;
    if (db == null || ownerId == null) return;
    _write(db, ownerId, type, details);
  }

  Future<void> _write(AppDatabase db, String ownerId, ActivityActionType type, String details) async {
    try {
      await db.into(db.activityLogTable).insert(ActivityLogTableCompanion.insert(
            id: generateUuidV4(),
            ownerId: ownerId,
            occurredAt: DateTime.now(),
            actionType: activityActionTypeToRow(type),
            details: Value(details),
            synced: const Value(false),
            updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
          ));
      if (_syncEngine != null) {
        await _syncEngine.pushPendingActivityLog(ownerId);
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // Offline or transient failure — see class doc comment.
    }
  }
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\auth\auth_service.dart =====
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// Thin wrapper around Supabase Auth — every tenant-scoped Drift row
/// needs a real `ownerId`, and this is the one place that knows how to
/// get one. See design/APP_CONTEXT.md "Multi-Tenant & Admin
/// Architecture": `profiles.role` (fetched here after sign-in) is what
/// gates the admin section, on top of the owner_id every table carries.
class AuthService {
  final SupabaseClient _client;

  AuthService(this._client);

  /// The logged-in seller/admin's id — every tenant-scoped Drift write
  /// must be tagged with this. Null when signed out.
  String? get currentOwnerId => _client.auth.currentUser?.id;

  bool get isSignedIn => _client.auth.currentUser != null;

  Future<void> signIn({required String email, required String password}) {
    return _client.auth.signInWithPassword(email: email, password: password);
  }

  Future<void> signOut() => _client.auth.signOut();

  /// Re-authenticates with [currentPassword] to verify it's correct
  /// (Supabase has no standalone "verify password" call — signing in
  /// again is the standard way), then updates to [newPassword]. Throws
  /// an `AuthException` from the re-auth step if the current password
  /// is wrong, before ever touching the real password.
  Future<void> changePassword({required String currentPassword, required String newPassword}) async {
    final email = _client.auth.currentUser?.email;
    if (email == null) throw StateError('No signed-in user');
    await _client.auth.signInWithPassword(email: email, password: currentPassword);
    await _client.auth.updateUser(UserAttributes(password: newPassword));
  }

  /// `'seller'` or `'admin'` — read fresh from `profiles` rather than
  /// cached, since an admin can change a role while this session is
  /// still active.
  Future<String> fetchCurrentRole() async {
    final userId = currentOwnerId;
    if (userId == null) throw StateError('No signed-in user');
    final row = await _client.from('profiles').select('role').eq('id', userId).single();
    return row['role'] as String;
  }

  /// Calls the `admin-manage-user` Edge Function (service-role account
  /// management — create/activate/deactivate a seller, change a role).
  /// The function itself re-verifies the caller is an active admin via
  /// their own JWT before doing anything privileged; this client never
  /// holds a service-role key. Throws with the function's own error
  /// message on failure (e.g. "Admin role required", a 403).
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> callAdminManageUser(Map<String, dynamic> body) async {
    final response = await _client.functions.invoke('admin-manage-user', body: body);
    final data = response.data;
    if (response.status >= 400) {
      final message = data is Map && data['error'] != null ? data['error'].toString() : 'Request failed (${response.status})';
      throw Exception(message);
    }
    return data is Map<String, dynamic> ? data : <String, dynamic>{};
  }
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\catalog\company_catalog.dart =====
import 'company_summary.dart';

/// The read/write surface the Companies feature exposes to other
/// features (New Sale, Shipments & Operations) — a narrow interface so
/// those features depend on a core-level contract instead of importing
/// Companies' Cubit/entities directly (see CLAUDE.md: no cross-feature
/// imports except through core/). Implemented by `CompaniesCubit`.
abstract class CompanyCatalog {
  List<CompanySummary> get activeCompanies;

  /// Fires whenever [activeCompanies] may have changed — including the
  /// cubit's own async initial load finishing (Drift/Supabase reads
  /// resolve after construction), so a reader built before that load
  /// completed (Home, on a cold app start) can react instead of staying
  /// stuck on an empty/zeroed snapshot until something else happens to
  /// call it again.
  Stream<void> get changes;

  /// Records a sale against a company's shipment balance: increases
  /// consumed, decreases available-to-sell, bumps operations count and
  /// profit generated.
  void recordSale({required String companyId, required double wholesaleCost, required double profit});

  /// Undoes a previously-recorded sale — call when the Line it was
  /// recorded for is deleted, or edited to a different company/cost
  /// (reverse the old values, then `recordSale` the new ones). Exact
  /// inverse of [recordSale]; without this, a company's consumed/
  /// available balance would never reflect a Line going away until the
  /// next full app reload recomputes it from scratch.
  void reverseSale({required String companyId, required double wholesaleCost, required double profit});

  /// Records a new shipment (balance top-up) against a company: grows
  /// available-to-sell by [totalAmount], and whatever wasn't paid now
  /// ([totalAmount] - [amountPaid]) adds to what's owed to the company.
  void addShipment({required String companyId, required double totalAmount, required double amountPaid});

  /// Records an "Additional Service" data top-up's cost against a
  /// company's balance — increases consumed, decreases available-to-sell.
  /// Unlike [recordSale], does not bump operations count or profit
  /// generated (this isn't a new Line being sold).
  void recordAdditionalServiceCharge({required String companyId, required double amount});

  /// Undoes a previously-recorded [recordAdditionalServiceCharge] — call
  /// when the Line it was charged against gets deleted. Exact inverse.
  void reverseAdditionalServiceCharge({required String companyId, required double amount});
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

import '../number_update/number_update_provider.dart';

/// Read-only package snapshot exposed to other features through
/// [CompanyCatalog] — deliberately a plain DTO, not the Companies
/// feature's own `CompanyPackage` entity, so features stay decoupled.
class PackageSummary extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String? simType;
  final double wholesaleCost;
  final double retailPrice;

  const PackageSummary({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    this.simType,
    required this.wholesaleCost,
    required this.retailPrice,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [id, name, simType, wholesaleCost, retailPrice];
}

/// Read-only company snapshot exposed to other features through
/// [CompanyCatalog]. The financial fields default to 0 so existing call
/// sites that don't care about them (New Sale's company picker) don't
/// need updating — only Profits/Reports/Shipments read them.
class CompanySummary extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String initials;
  final List<PackageSummary> packages;
  final double profitGenerated;
  final int operationsCount;
  final int usagePercent;
  final double consumed;
  final double availableToSell;
  final double owedToCompany;
  final NumberUpdateProviderKey? numberUpdateProvider;
  final String? logoUrl;
  final String? numberUpdateUrl;

  /// See `Company.allowNegativeBalance`'s doc comment — carried through
  /// here so New Sale (the only consumer that needs it) can decide
  /// whether a sale exceeding [availableToSell] is still allowed.
  final bool allowNegativeBalance;

  const CompanySummary({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.initials,
    required this.packages,
    this.profitGenerated = 0,
    this.operationsCount = 0,
    this.usagePercent = 0,
    this.consumed = 0,
    this.availableToSell = 0,
    this.owedToCompany = 0,
    this.numberUpdateProvider,
    this.logoUrl,
    this.numberUpdateUrl,
    this.allowNegativeBalance = false,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        id,
        name,
        initials,
        packages,
        profitGenerated,
        operationsCount,
        usagePercent,
        consumed,
        availableToSell,
        owedToCompany,
        numberUpdateProvider,
        logoUrl,
        numberUpdateUrl,
        allowNegativeBalance,
      ];
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\catalog\customer_catalog.dart =====
import 'customer_summary.dart';
import 'line_summary.dart';

/// The read/write surface the Customers feature exposes to other
/// features (currently New Sale, Numbers) — see [CompanyCatalog] for why
/// this exists. Implemented by `CustomersCubit`.
abstract class CustomerCatalog {
  List<CustomerSummary> get activeCustomers;

  /// Every sold Line/SIM across every customer — used by the Numbers
  /// page, which is a global log rather than a per-customer view.
  List<LineSummary> get allLines;

  /// Fires whenever [activeCustomers]/[allLines] may have changed —
  /// including the cubit's own async initial load finishing (see
  /// [CompanyCatalog.changes] for why this matters).
  Stream<void> get changes;

  /// Quick customer creation from within the New Sale flow (name +
  /// contact only — full editing still happens on the Customers page).
  /// Returns the new customer's id.
  String quickAddCustomer({required String name, required String contactNumber});

  /// Adds a sold Line under an existing customer. Payment status is
  /// derived internally from `amountPaid` vs `retailPrice`. [companyId]
  /// (and [packageId], when the sale used a real catalog/company
  /// package rather than a free-typed price) are what let this Line
  /// persist for real once Lines are backed by Drift — `companyName`/
  /// `companyInitials`/`packageName` stay purely for display so
  /// existing widgets (LineCard, Numbers table, ...) don't need to look
  /// the company back up just to render a row.
  void addSaleLine({
    required String customerId,
    required String simNumber,
    required String companyId,
    required String companyName,
    required String companyInitials,
    String? packageId,
    required String packageName,
    String? packageSimType,
    required DateTime purchaseDate,
    required DateTime expiryDate,
    required double wholesaleCost,
    required double retailPrice,
    required double amountPaid,
  });
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\catalog\customer_summary.dart =====
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

/// Decoupled from the Customers feature's own `CustomerStatus` enum, for
/// the same reason `LinePaymentStatus`/`LineHealthStatus` are decoupled
/// from `Line`'s enums (see `line_summary.dart`). `.label` calls `.tr()`
/// directly, so callers must compare the enum value itself, never the
/// label string, or comparisons break once the app runs in Arabic.
enum CustomerStatusSummary { normal, troubled, blocked }

extension CustomerStatusSummaryLabel on CustomerStatusSummary {
  String get label {
    switch (this) {
      case CustomerStatusSummary.normal:
        return 'customers.status.normal'.tr();
      case CustomerStatusSummary.troubled:
        return 'customers.status.troubled'.tr();
      case CustomerStatusSummary.blocked:
        return 'customers.status.blocked'.tr();
    }
  }
}

/// Read-only customer snapshot exposed to other features through
/// [CustomerCatalog] — a plain DTO, not the Customers feature's own
/// `Customer` entity, so features stay decoupled.
class CustomerSummary extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String contactNumber;
  final CustomerStatusSummary status;

  const CustomerSummary({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.contactNumber,
    required this.status,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [id, name, contactNumber, status];
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\catalog\line_summary.dart =====
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

enum LineHealthStatus { active, expiringSoon, expired }

extension LineHealthStatusLabel on LineHealthStatus {
  String get label {
    switch (this) {
      case LineHealthStatus.active:
        return 'common.lineHealthStatus.active'.tr();
      case LineHealthStatus.expiringSoon:
        return 'common.lineHealthStatus.expiringSoon'.tr();
      case LineHealthStatus.expired:
        return 'common.lineHealthStatus.expired'.tr();
    }
  }
}

enum LinePaymentStatus { paid, partial, unpaid }

extension LinePaymentStatusLabel on LinePaymentStatus {
  String get label {
    switch (this) {
      case LinePaymentStatus.paid:
        return 'common.paymentStatus.paid'.tr();
      case LinePaymentStatus.partial:
        return 'common.paymentStatus.partial'.tr();
      case LinePaymentStatus.unpaid:
        return 'common.paymentStatus.unpaid'.tr();
    }
  }
}

/// Read-only line/SIM snapshot exposed to other features (Numbers,
/// Profits, Reports) through [CustomerCatalog] — a plain DTO, not the
/// Customers feature's own `Line` entity/`PaymentStatus` enum, so
/// features stay decoupled. Financial/customer fields default to
/// neutral values so existing call sites that don't care about them
/// (Numbers) don't need updating.
class LineSummary extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String customerName;
  final String customerContact;

  /// True when the customer this line belongs to is soft-deleted (or no
  /// longer exists at all) — set by `CustomersCubit.allLines`. Numbers/
  /// Profits/Reports keep showing these lines (a completed sale's
  /// history/profit doesn't stop being real just because the customer
  /// record was later deleted), but any "still owed"/"to collect" total
  /// built from [amountRemaining] should exclude them — that debt is no
  /// longer tracked/collectible through the app once its customer is
  /// gone, and leaving it in would show a seller a mystery balance with
  /// no customer left to explain or act on it.
  final bool customerDeleted;

  final String simNumber;

  /// Real FK into the seller's own `companies` — see `Line`'s doc
  /// comment for why this and `companyName` both exist. Defaults to ''
  /// only so old call sites that predate this field don't need touching;
  /// the one real implementer (`CustomersCubit.allLines`) always sets it.
  final String companyId;
  final String companyName;
  final String companyInitials;
  final String packageName;
  final String? packageSimType;
  final DateTime purchaseDate;
  final DateTime expiryDate;
  final double wholesaleCost;
  final double retailPrice;
  final double amountPaid;
  final double amountRemaining;
  final LinePaymentStatus paymentStatus;

  const LineSummary({
    required this.id,
    required this.simNumber,
    this.companyId = '',
    required this.companyName,
    required this.companyInitials,
    required this.packageName,
    this.packageSimType,
    required this.purchaseDate,
    required this.expiryDate,
    this.customerName = '',
    this.customerContact = '',
    this.customerDeleted = false,
    this.wholesaleCost = 0,
    this.retailPrice = 0,
    this.amountPaid = 0,
    this.amountRemaining = 0,
    this.paymentStatus = LinePaymentStatus.paid,
  });

  double get profit => retailPrice - wholesaleCost;

  LineHealthStatus statusAt(DateTime now) {
    if (expiryDate.isBefore(now)) return LineHealthStatus.expired;
    if (expiryDate.difference(now).inDays <= 7) return LineHealthStatus.expiringSoon;
    return LineHealthStatus.active;
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        id,
        customerName,
        customerContact,
        customerDeleted,
        simNumber,
        companyId,
        companyName,
        companyInitials,
        packageName,
        packageSimType,
        purchaseDate,
        expiryDate,
        wholesaleCost,
        retailPrice,
        amountPaid,
        amountRemaining,
        paymentStatus,
      ];
}

/// Custom URL scheme the app registers on Android (`AndroidManifest.xml`
/// intent-filter) and iOS (`Info.plist` `CFBundleURLTypes`) so a Supabase
/// Auth "reset password" email link can reopen this app instead of a
/// browser. `supabase_flutter` already listens for and processes this
/// deep link internally (via its bundled `app_links` dependency) — it
/// exchanges the link for a session and fires
/// `AuthChangeEvent.passwordRecovery`, which `main.dart` listens for to
/// push the "set new password" screen.
class DeepLinks {
  DeepLinks._();

  static const resetPasswordCallback = 'simsalesmanager://reset-callback';
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\config\env.dart =====
import 'dart:io';

/// Supabase project credentials. Defaulted below so a plain `flutter
/// run` / `flutter build apk --release` — no extra flags — just works,
/// same as any other app. This is safe to hardcode: it's the
/// *publishable* anon key, not a secret — Supabase's real access control
/// is Postgres RLS policies on the server, and this key is designed to
/// ship inside every client app's compiled binary the same way.
/// `--dart-define` still overrides these if ever needed (e.g. pointing a
/// build at a different Supabase project):
///
///   flutter run --dart-define=SUPABASE_URL=... --dart-define=SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=...
///
/// [_isFlutterTest] checks the real OS process environment variable the
/// `flutter test` command sets on every test run (`FLUTTER_TEST=true`) —
/// not a `--dart-define` (a `flutter test` invocation never passes those,
/// so a `bool.fromEnvironment` check here would always read `false` and
/// can't tell a test run apart from a real one). Reading it via
/// `Platform.environment` at runtime instead forces [isConfigured] false
/// specifically under `flutter test`, so the whole suite keeps running
/// against predictable seeded mock data with zero setup, exactly as
/// before, while `run`/`build` get the real defaults with no flags
/// needed.
class Env {
  static bool get _isFlutterTest => Platform.environment.containsKey('FLUTTER_TEST');

  static const supabaseUrl = String.fromEnvironment(
    'SUPABASE_URL',
    defaultValue: 'https://bgieiaxbfoosbgemrppp.supabase.co',
  );
  static const supabaseAnonKey = String.fromEnvironment(
    'SUPABASE_ANON_KEY',
    defaultValue: 'sb_publishable_iEjZSk-cM95tB8gAxIw0Kw_z2d_gUbM',
  );

  static bool get isConfigured => !_isFlutterTest && supabaseUrl.isNotEmpty && supabaseAnonKey.isNotEmpty;
}

import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';

/// Shared period-filter options reused across Home, Profits, Reports,
/// Numbers, and Shipments & Operations pages.
enum PeriodFilter {
  all,
  today,
  thisWeek,
  thisMonth,
  threeMonths,
  sixMonths,
  thisYear,
  custom,
}

extension PeriodFilterLabel on PeriodFilter {
  /// `tr()` works without a BuildContext once `EasyLocalization` is mounted
  /// anywhere in the tree, which is convenient here — this label is used
  /// from plain data mapping (`.map((p) => p.label)`), not widget build.
  String get label {
    switch (this) {
      case PeriodFilter.all:
        return 'periodFilter.all'.tr();
      case PeriodFilter.today:
        return 'periodFilter.today'.tr();
      case PeriodFilter.thisWeek:
        return 'periodFilter.thisWeek'.tr();
      case PeriodFilter.thisMonth:
        return 'periodFilter.thisMonth'.tr();
      case PeriodFilter.threeMonths:
        return 'periodFilter.threeMonths'.tr();
      case PeriodFilter.sixMonths:
        return 'periodFilter.sixMonths'.tr();
      case PeriodFilter.thisYear:
        return 'periodFilter.thisYear'.tr();
      case PeriodFilter.custom:
        return 'periodFilter.custom'.tr();
    }
  }
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\database\app_database.dart =====
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:drift/native.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';

import 'tables/activity_log_table.dart';
import 'tables/additional_services_table.dart';
import 'tables/catalog_tables.dart';
import 'tables/companies_table.dart';
import 'tables/customers_table.dart';
import 'tables/lines_table.dart';
import 'tables/number_update_log_table.dart';
import 'tables/notifications_table.dart';
import 'tables/number_update_provider_cache_table.dart';
import 'tables/operations_table.dart';
import 'tables/packages_table.dart';
import 'tables/pending_deletes_table.dart';
import 'tables/settings_table.dart';
import 'tables/shipments_table.dart';
import 'tables/support_requests_table.dart';

part 'app_database.g.dart';

/// The offline-first source of truth (CLAUDE.md) — every feature reads
/// and writes here first; the sync engine in `core/sync/` reconciles this
/// with Supabase in the background. One database file per device install,
/// holding whichever single account is currently logged in plus a
/// read-only mirror of the shared company/package catalog.
@DriftDatabase(
  tables: [
    CompanyCatalogTable,
    PackageCatalogTable,
    CompaniesTable,
    PackagesTable,
    CustomersTable,
    ShipmentsTable,
    LinesTable,
    OperationsTable,
    ActivityLogTable,
    SupportRequestsTable,
    SettingsTable,
    NumberUpdateLogTable,
    NumberUpdateProviderCacheTable,
    PendingDeletesTable,
    NotificationsTable,
    AdditionalServicesTable,
  ],
)
class AppDatabase extends _$AppDatabase {
  AppDatabase() : super(_openConnection());

  AppDatabase.forTesting(super.executor);

  // `schemaVersion` sat at 1 for this app's entire build-out even as
  // columns/tables kept getting added — meaning a device that installed
  // the app early (this app's only real device so far) never actually
  // got those additions applied to its physical SQLite file: Drift only
  // runs `onCreate`/`onUpgrade` when the declared version changes, and
  // it never did. The generated code (this file) always reflects the
  // *current* schema regardless, so every app rebuild silently drifted
  // further from what was actually sitting on disk — until a query
  // finally touched one of the missing pieces and failed outright
  // (surfacing as sync going straight to its error state on every
  // retry: the local `companies` SELECT/INSERT itself was throwing
  // "no such column", so nothing — including an unsynced local change
  // like a freshly-picked logo — ever got as far as attempting the
  // network call).
  //
  // Bumping to 2 and adding exactly the pieces introduced since v1
  // heals an existing install without touching any data already
  // there (including anything still only-local and unsynced) — this is
  // additive only, nothing here can lose data.
  @override
  int get schemaVersion => 12;

  @override
  MigrationStrategy get migration => MigrationStrategy(
        onCreate: (Migrator m) => m.createAll(),
        onUpgrade: (Migrator m, int from, int to) async {
          if (from < 2) {
            await m.addColumn(companiesTable, companiesTable.numberUpdateProvider);
            await m.addColumn(companiesTable, companiesTable.numberUpdateUrl);
            await m.addColumn(companyCatalogTable, companyCatalogTable.numberUpdateProvider);
            await m.createTable(numberUpdateLogTable);
          }
          if (from < 3) {
            // See lines_table.dart's doc comment on these columns for
            // why a Line needs its own frozen company/package name
            // snapshot instead of always live-joining the current
            // Companies list.
            await m.addColumn(linesTable, linesTable.companyName);
            await m.addColumn(linesTable, linesTable.companyInitials);
            await m.addColumn(linesTable, linesTable.packageName);
          }
          if (from < 4) {
            // See settings_table.dart's doc comment on this column —
            // a local-only cache, nothing to add server-side.
            await m.addColumn(settingsTable, settingsTable.cachedDisplayName);
          }
          if (from < 5) {
            // See number_update_provider_cache_table.dart's doc comment —
            // remembers which supplier a number matched last time.
            await m.createTable(numberUpdateProviderCacheTable);
          }
          if (from < 6) {
            // "SIM type" — see CompanyPackage's doc comment.
            await m.addColumn(packagesTable, packagesTable.simType);
            await m.addColumn(packageCatalogTable, packageCatalogTable.simType);
            await m.addColumn(linesTable, linesTable.packageSimType);
          }
          if (from < 7) {
            // `lines.companyId` NOT NULL -> nullable — see
            // lines_table.dart's doc comment: a permanently-deleted
            // company now detaches from its historical Lines instead of
            // deleting them. SQLite can't ALTER a column's nullability
            // directly, so this recreates the table per Drift's own
            // 12-step procedure (`alterTable`) — existing data is
            // preserved, nothing here can lose rows.
            await m.alterTable(TableMigration(linesTable));
          }
          if (from < 8) {
            // See pending_deletes_table.dart's doc comment — tombstones
            // a permanent delete until its remote counterpart is
            // confirmed, so a pull that races ahead of it can't
            // resurrect the row.
            await m.createTable(pendingDeletesTable);
          }
          if (from < 9) {
            // See settings_table.dart's doc comment on `cachedRole` —
            // lets a cold app start route a signed-in admin to the
            // right shell while offline, instead of only being able to
            // find that out with a network call.
            await m.addColumn(settingsTable, settingsTable.cachedRole);
          }
          if (from < 10) {
            // See companies_table.dart's doc comment on
            // allowNegativeBalance — the "allow negative balance"
            // per-company opt-in.
            await m.addColumn(companiesTable, companiesTable.allowNegativeBalance);
          }
          if (from < 11) {
            // See notifications_table.dart's doc comment — the
            // notifications feature's local-only inbox + scheduling
            // source of truth.
            await m.createTable(notificationsTable);
          }
          if (from < 12) {
            // See additional_services_table.dart's doc comment — the
            // "Additional Service" per-line data top-up feature's
            // history/ledger table.
            await m.createTable(additionalServicesTable);
          }
        },
      );
}

QueryExecutor _openConnection() {
  return LazyDatabase(() async {
    final dbFolder = await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();
    final file = File('${dbFolder.path}${Platform.pathSeparator}sim_sales_manager.sqlite');
    return NativeDatabase.createInBackground(file);
  });
}

        json['suggestedRetailPrice'],
      ),

      createdAt: serializer.fromJson<DateTime>(json['createdAt']),
      updatedAt: serializer.fromJson<DateTime>(json['updatedAt']),
    );
  }

class CompaniesTableData extends DataClass
    implements Insertable<CompaniesTableData> {
  final String ownerId;
  final bool synced;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  final String name;
  final String? logoUrl;
  final String? notes;
  final String status;
  final bool pinned;
  final String? catalogCompanyId;
  final String? numberUpdateProvider;
  final String? numberUpdateUrl;
  final bool allowNegativeBalance;
  const CompaniesTableData({
    required this.ownerId,
    required this.synced,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    this.logoUrl,
    this.notes,
    required this.status,
    required this.pinned,

    this.numberUpdateProvider,
    this.numberUpdateUrl,
    required this.allowNegativeBalance,
  });
  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId);
    map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced);
    map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt);
    map['id'] = Variable<String>(id);
    map['name'] = Variable<String>(name);
    if (!nullToAbsent || logoUrl != null) {
      map['logo_url'] = Variable<String>(logoUrl);
    }
    if (!nullToAbsent || notes != null) {
      map['notes'] = Variable<String>(notes);
    }
    map['status'] = Variable<String>(status);
    map['pinned'] = Variable<bool>(pinned);
    if (!nullToAbsent || catalogCompanyId != null) {
      map['catalog_company_id'] = Variable<String>(catalogCompanyId);
    }
    if (!nullToAbsent || numberUpdateProvider != null) {
      map['number_update_provider'] = Variable<String>(numberUpdateProvider);
    }
    if (!nullToAbsent || numberUpdateUrl != null) {
      map['number_update_url'] = Variable<String>(numberUpdateUrl);
    }
    map['allow_negative_balance'] = Variable<bool>(allowNegativeBalance);
    return map;
  }

  CompaniesTableCompanion toCompanion(bool nullToAbsent) {
    return CompaniesTableCompanion(
      ownerId: Value(ownerId),
      synced: Value(synced),
      updatedAt: Value(updatedAt),

      name: Value(name),
      logoUrl: logoUrl == null && nullToAbsent

    );
  }

    Expression<String>? ownerId,

    Expression<String>? id,

    Expression<String>? simType,

  }) {

      if (id != null) 'id': id,
      if (companyId != null) 'company_id': companyId,

      if (simType != null) 'sim_type': simType,

      if (rowid != null) 'rowid': rowid,
    });
  }

    Insertable<CustomersTableData> instance, {

    final context = VerificationContext();
    final data = instance.toColumns(true);
    if (data.containsKey('owner_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _ownerIdMeta,
        ownerId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['owner_id']!, _ownerIdMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_ownerIdMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('synced')) {
      context.handle(
        _syncedMeta,
        synced.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['synced']!, _syncedMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('updated_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _updatedAtMeta,
        updatedAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['updated_at']!, _updatedAtMeta),

      context.missing(_idMeta);
    }

      context.handle(
        _nameMeta,
        name.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['name']!, _nameMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_nameMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('contact_number')) {
      context.handle(
        _contactNumberMeta,
        contactNumber.isAcceptableOrUnknown(
          data['contact_number']!,
          _contactNumberMeta,
        ),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_contactNumberMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('status')) {
      context.handle(
        _statusMeta,
        status.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['status']!, _statusMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('tags')) {
      context.handle(
        _tagsMeta,
        tags.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['tags']!, _tagsMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('pinned')) {
      context.handle(
        _pinnedMeta,
        pinned.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['pinned']!, _pinnedMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('notes')) {
      context.handle(
        _notesMeta,
        notes.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['notes']!, _notesMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('deleted')) {
      context.handle(
        _deletedMeta,
        deleted.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['deleted']!, _deletedMeta),
      );
    }
    return context;
  }

  @override
  Set<GeneratedColumn> get $primaryKey => {id};
  @override

class CustomersTableData extends DataClass
    implements Insertable<CustomersTableData> {
  final String ownerId;
  final bool synced;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String contactNumber;
  final String status;

  /// Comma-joined free-text tags — mirrors Postgres' `text[]`. A `,` in a
  /// tag itself isn't supported, consistent with tags being short labels
  /// picked from quick suggestions rather than free sentences.
  final String tags;
  final bool pinned;
  final String? notes;
  final bool deleted;
  const CustomersTableData({
    required this.ownerId,
    required this.synced,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.contactNumber,
    required this.status,
    required this.tags,
    required this.pinned,
    this.notes,
    required this.deleted,
  });
  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId);
    map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced);
    map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt);
    map['id'] = Variable<String>(id);
    map['name'] = Variable<String>(name);
    map['contact_number'] = Variable<String>(contactNumber);
    map['status'] = Variable<String>(status);
    map['tags'] = Variable<String>(tags);
    map['pinned'] = Variable<bool>(pinned);
    if (!nullToAbsent || notes != null) {
      map['notes'] = Variable<String>(notes);
    }
    map['deleted'] = Variable<bool>(deleted);
    return map;
  }

  CustomersTableCompanion toCompanion(bool nullToAbsent) {
    return CustomersTableCompanion(
      ownerId: Value(ownerId),
      synced: Value(synced),
      updatedAt: Value(updatedAt),
      id: Value(id),
      name: Value(name),
      contactNumber: Value(contactNumber),
      status: Value(status),
      tags: Value(tags),
      pinned: Value(pinned),
      notes: notes == null && nullToAbsent
          ? const Value.absent()
          : Value(notes),
      deleted: Value(deleted),
    );
  }

  factory CustomersTableData.fromJson(
    Map<String, dynamic> json, {
    ValueSerializer? serializer,
  }) {
    serializer ??= driftRuntimeOptions.defaultSerializer;
    return CustomersTableData(
      ownerId: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['ownerId']),
      synced: serializer.fromJson<bool>(json['synced']),
      updatedAt: serializer.fromJson<DateTime>(json['updatedAt']),
      id: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['id']),
      name: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['name']),
      contactNumber: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['contactNumber']),
      status: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['status']),
      tags: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['tags']),
      pinned: serializer.fromJson<bool>(json['pinned']),
      notes: serializer.fromJson<String?>(json['notes']),
      deleted: serializer.fromJson<bool>(json['deleted']),
    );
  }
  @override
  Map<String, dynamic> toJson({ValueSerializer? serializer}) {
    serializer ??= driftRuntimeOptions.defaultSerializer;
    return <String, dynamic>{
      'ownerId': serializer.toJson<String>(ownerId),
      'synced': serializer.toJson<bool>(synced),
      'updatedAt': serializer.toJson<DateTime>(updatedAt),
      'id': serializer.toJson<String>(id),
      'name': serializer.toJson<String>(name),
      'contactNumber': serializer.toJson<String>(contactNumber),
      'status': serializer.toJson<String>(status),
      'tags': serializer.toJson<String>(tags),
      'pinned': serializer.toJson<bool>(pinned),
      'notes': serializer.toJson<String?>(notes),
      'deleted': serializer.toJson<bool>(deleted),
    };
  }

  CustomersTableData copyWith({
    String? ownerId,
    bool? synced,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? contactNumber,
    String? status,
    String? tags,

    Value<String?> notes = const Value.absent(),
    bool? deleted,
  }) => CustomersTableData(

    updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,

    name: name ?? this.name,
    contactNumber: contactNumber ?? this.contactNumber,
    status: status ?? this.status,
    tags: tags ?? this.tags,
    pinned: pinned ?? this.pinned,
    notes: notes.present ? notes.value : this.notes,
    deleted: deleted ?? this.deleted,
  );
  CustomersTableData copyWithCompanion(CustomersTableCompanion data) {
    return CustomersTableData(
      ownerId: data.ownerId.present ? data.ownerId.value : this.ownerId,
      synced: data.synced.present ? data.synced.value : this.synced,
      updatedAt: data.updatedAt.present ? data.updatedAt.value : this.updatedAt,
      id: data.id.present ? data.id.value : this.id,
      name: data.name.present ? data.name.value : this.name,
      contactNumber: data.contactNumber.present
          ? data.contactNumber.value
          : this.contactNumber,
      status: data.status.present ? data.status.value : this.status,
      tags: data.tags.present ? data.tags.value : this.tags,
      pinned: data.pinned.present ? data.pinned.value : this.pinned,
      notes: data.notes.present ? data.notes.value : this.notes,
      deleted: data.deleted.present ? data.deleted.value : this.deleted,
    );
  }

          ..write('ownerId: $ownerId, ')
          ..write('synced: $synced, ')
          ..write('updatedAt: $updatedAt, ')
          ..write('id: $id, ')

          ..write('pinned: $pinned, ')
          ..write('notes: $notes, ')
          ..write('deleted: $deleted')
          ..write(')'))
        .toString();
  }

  int get hashCode => Object.hash(
    ownerId,
    synced,
    updatedAt,
    id,
    name,
    contactNumber,
    status,
    tags,
    pinned,
    notes,
    deleted,
  );

  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      identical(this, other) ||
      (other is CustomersTableData &&
          other.ownerId == this.ownerId &&
          other.synced == this.synced &&
          other.updatedAt == this.updatedAt &&
          other.id == this.id &&
          other.name == this.name &&
          other.contactNumber == this.contactNumber &&
          other.status == this.status &&
          other.tags == this.tags &&
          other.pinned == this.pinned &&
          other.notes == this.notes &&
          other.deleted == this.deleted);

  VerificationContext validateIntegrity(
    Insertable<LinesTableData> instance, {
    bool isInserting = false,
  }) {
    final context = VerificationContext();
    final data = instance.toColumns(true);
    if (data.containsKey('owner_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _ownerIdMeta,
        ownerId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['owner_id']!, _ownerIdMeta),

      );
    }

        _simNumberMeta,
        simNumber.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['sim_number']!, _simNumberMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_simNumberMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('company_name')) {
      context.handle(
        _companyNameMeta,
        companyName.isAcceptableOrUnknown(
          data['company_name']!,
          _companyNameMeta,
        ),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('company_initials')) {
      context.handle(

  });
  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId);
    map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced);
    map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt);
    map['id'] = Variable<String>(id);
    map['customer_id'] = Variable<String>(customerId);
    if (!nullToAbsent || companyId != null) {
      map['company_id'] = Variable<String>(companyId);
    }
    if (!nullToAbsent || packageId != null) {
      map['package_id'] = Variable<String>(packageId);
    }
    map['sim_number'] = Variable<String>(simNumber);
    if (!nullToAbsent || companyName != null) {
      map['company_name'] = Variable<String>(companyName);
    }
    if (!nullToAbsent || companyInitials != null) {
      map['company_initials'] = Variable<String>(companyInitials);
    }
    if (!nullToAbsent || packageName != null) {
      map['package_name'] = Variable<String>(packageName);
    }
    if (!nullToAbsent || packageSimType != null) {
      map['package_sim_type'] = Variable<String>(packageSimType);
    }
    map['purchase_date'] = Variable<DateTime>(purchaseDate);
    map['expiry_date'] = Variable<DateTime>(expiryDate);
    map['wholesale_cost'] = Variable<double>(wholesaleCost);
    map['retail_price'] = Variable<double>(retailPrice);
    map['profit'] = Variable<double>(profit);
    map['payment_status'] = Variable<String>(paymentStatus);
    map['amount_paid'] = Variable<double>(amountPaid);
    map['amount_remaining'] = Variable<double>(amountRemaining);
    return map;
  }

          ? data.amountPaid.value
          : this.amountPaid,
      amountRemaining: data.amountRemaining.present
          ? data.amountRemaining.value

    );

class OperationsTableData extends DataClass
    implements Insertable<OperationsTableData> {
  final String ownerId;
  final bool synced;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  final String id;
  final String customerId;
  final String? lineId;
  final String companyId;
  final DateTime occurredAt;
  final double salePrice;
  final double cost;
  final double profit;
  final String paymentStatus;
  final String? repName;
  const OperationsTableData({
    required this.ownerId,
    required this.synced,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.id,
    required this.customerId,
    this.lineId,
    required this.companyId,
    required this.occurredAt,
    required this.salePrice,
    required this.cost,
    required this.profit,
    required this.paymentStatus,
    this.repName,
  });
  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId);
    map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced);
    map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt);
    map['id'] = Variable<String>(id);
    map['customer_id'] = Variable<String>(customerId);
    if (!nullToAbsent || lineId != null) {
      map['line_id'] = Variable<String>(lineId);
    }
    map['company_id'] = Variable<String>(companyId);
    map['occurred_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(occurredAt);
    map['sale_price'] = Variable<double>(salePrice);
    map['cost'] = Variable<double>(cost);
    map['profit'] = Variable<double>(profit);
    map['payment_status'] = Variable<String>(paymentStatus);
    if (!nullToAbsent || repName != null) {
      map['rep_name'] = Variable<String>(repName);
    }
    return map;
  }

  OperationsTableCompanion toCompanion(bool nullToAbsent) {
    return OperationsTableCompanion(
      ownerId: Value(ownerId),
      synced: Value(synced),
      updatedAt: Value(updatedAt),
      id: Value(id),
      customerId: Value(customerId),
      lineId: lineId == null && nullToAbsent
          ? const Value.absent()
          : Value(lineId),
      companyId: Value(companyId),
      occurredAt: Value(occurredAt),
      salePrice: Value(salePrice),
      cost: Value(cost),
      profit: Value(profit),
      paymentStatus: Value(paymentStatus),
      repName: repName == null && nullToAbsent

    'ownerId',

  );

    'synced',

    'updatedAt',
  );
  @override
  late final GeneratedColumn<DateTime> updatedAt = GeneratedColumn<DateTime>(
    'updated_at',
    aliasedName,
    false,
    type: DriftSqlType.dateTime,
    requiredDuringInsert: true,
  );

    aliasedName,

    type: DriftSqlType.string,
    requiredDuringInsert: true,
  );

    'occurredAt',
  );

    'occurred_at',
    aliasedName,
    false,
    type: DriftSqlType.dateTime,
    requiredDuringInsert: true,
  );
  static const VerificationMeta _actionTypeMeta = const VerificationMeta(
    'actionType',
  );
  @override
  late final GeneratedColumn<String> actionType = GeneratedColumn<String>(
    'action_type',
    aliasedName,
    false,
    type: DriftSqlType.string,
    requiredDuringInsert: true,
  );

    'details',
  );
  @override
  late final GeneratedColumn<String> details = GeneratedColumn<String>(
    'details',
    aliasedName,
    true,
    type: DriftSqlType.string,
    requiredDuringInsert: false,
  );
  @override
  List<GeneratedColumn> get $columns => [
    ownerId,
    synced,
    updatedAt,
    id,
    occurredAt,
    actionType,
    details,
  ];
  @override
  String get aliasedName => _alias ?? actualTableName;
  @override
  String get actualTableName => $name;
  static const String $name = 'activity_log';
  @override
  VerificationContext validateIntegrity(
    Insertable<ActivityLogTableData> instance, {
    bool isInserting = false,
  }) {
    final context = VerificationContext();
    final data = instance.toColumns(true);
    if (data.containsKey('owner_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _ownerIdMeta,
        ownerId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['owner_id']!, _ownerIdMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_ownerIdMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('synced')) {
      context.handle(
        _syncedMeta,
        synced.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['synced']!, _syncedMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('updated_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _updatedAtMeta,
        updatedAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['updated_at']!, _updatedAtMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_updatedAtMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('id')) {
      context.handle(_idMeta, id.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['id']!, _idMeta));
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_idMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('occurred_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _occurredAtMeta,
        occurredAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['occurred_at']!, _occurredAtMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_occurredAtMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('action_type')) {
      context.handle(
        _actionTypeMeta,
        actionType.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['action_type']!, _actionTypeMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_actionTypeMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('details')) {
      context.handle(
        _detailsMeta,
        details.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['details']!, _detailsMeta),
      );
    }
    return context;
  }

  @override
  Set<GeneratedColumn> get $primaryKey => {id};
  @override
  ActivityLogTableData map(Map<String, dynamic> data, {String? tablePrefix}) {
    final effectivePrefix = tablePrefix != null ? '$tablePrefix.' : '';
    return ActivityLogTableData(
      ownerId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}owner_id'],
      )!,
      synced: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.bool,
        data['${effectivePrefix}synced'],

      if (details != null) 'details': details,
      if (rowid != null) 'rowid': rowid,
    });
  }

  ActivityLogTableCompanion copyWith({
    Value<String>? ownerId,
    Value<bool>? synced,
    Value<DateTime>? updatedAt,
    Value<String>? id,
    Value<DateTime>? occurredAt,
    Value<String>? actionType,
    Value<String?>? details,
    Value<int>? rowid,
  }) {
    return ActivityLogTableCompanion(
      ownerId: ownerId ?? this.ownerId,
      synced: synced ?? this.synced,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
      id: id ?? this.id,
      occurredAt: occurredAt ?? this.occurredAt,
      actionType: actionType ?? this.actionType,
      details: details ?? this.details,
      rowid: rowid ?? this.rowid,
    );
  }

  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    if (ownerId.present) {
      map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId.value);
    }
    if (synced.present) {
      map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced.value);
    }
    if (updatedAt.present) {
      map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt.value);
    }
    if (id.present) {
      map['id'] = Variable<String>(id.value);
    }
    if (occurredAt.present) {
      map['occurred_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(occurredAt.value);
    }
    if (actionType.present) {
      map['action_type'] = Variable<String>(actionType.value);
    }
    if (details.present) {
      map['details'] = Variable<String>(details.value);
    }
    if (rowid.present) {
      map['rowid'] = Variable<int>(rowid.value);
    }
    return map;
  }

  @override
  String toString() {
    return (StringBuffer('ActivityLogTableCompanion(')
          ..write('ownerId: $ownerId, ')
          ..write('synced: $synced, ')
          ..write('updatedAt: $updatedAt, ')
          ..write('id: $id, ')
          ..write('occurredAt: $occurredAt, ')
          ..write('actionType: $actionType, ')
          ..write('details: $details, ')
          ..write('rowid: $rowid')
          ..write(')'))
        .toString();
  }
}

class SupportRequestsTableData extends DataClass
    implements Insertable<SupportRequestsTableData> {
  final String ownerId;
  final bool synced;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  final String id;
  final String type;
  final String body;
  final String? attachmentUrl;
  final String? voiceNoteUrl;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  const SupportRequestsTableData({
    required this.ownerId,
    required this.synced,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.id,
    required this.type,
    required this.body,
    this.attachmentUrl,
    this.voiceNoteUrl,
    required this.createdAt,
  });
  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId);
    map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced);
    map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt);
    map['id'] = Variable<String>(id);
    map['type'] = Variable<String>(type);
    map['body'] = Variable<String>(body);
    if (!nullToAbsent || attachmentUrl != null) {
      map['attachment_url'] = Variable<String>(attachmentUrl);
    }
    if (!nullToAbsent || voiceNoteUrl != null) {
      map['voice_note_url'] = Variable<String>(voiceNoteUrl);
    }
    map['created_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(createdAt);
    return map;
  }

  SupportRequestsTableCompanion toCompanion(bool nullToAbsent) {
    return SupportRequestsTableCompanion(
      ownerId: Value(ownerId),
      synced: Value(synced),
      updatedAt: Value(updatedAt),
      id: Value(id),
      type: Value(type),
      body: Value(body),
      attachmentUrl: attachmentUrl == null && nullToAbsent
          ? const Value.absent()
          : Value(attachmentUrl),
      voiceNoteUrl: voiceNoteUrl == null && nullToAbsent
          ? const Value.absent()
          : Value(voiceNoteUrl),
      createdAt: Value(createdAt),
    );
  }

  factory SupportRequestsTableData.fromJson(
    Map<String, dynamic> json, {
    ValueSerializer? serializer,
  }) {
    serializer ??= driftRuntimeOptions.defaultSerializer;
    return SupportRequestsTableData(
      ownerId: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['ownerId']),
      synced: serializer.fromJson<bool>(json['synced']),
      updatedAt: serializer.fromJson<DateTime>(json['updatedAt']),

    );

    return <String, dynamic>{
      'ownerId': serializer.toJson<String>(ownerId),
      'synced': serializer.toJson<bool>(synced),
      'updatedAt': serializer.toJson<DateTime>(updatedAt),
      'id': serializer.toJson<String>(id),

        _cachedRoleMeta,
        cachedRole.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['cached_role']!, _cachedRoleMeta),
      );
    }
    return context;
  }

  @override
  Set<GeneratedColumn> get $primaryKey => {ownerId};
  @override
  SettingsTableData map(Map<String, dynamic> data, {String? tablePrefix}) {
    final effectivePrefix = tablePrefix != null ? '$tablePrefix.' : '';
    return SettingsTableData(
      ownerId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}owner_id'],
      )!,
      synced: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.bool,
        data['${effectivePrefix}synced'],
      )!,
      updatedAt: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.dateTime,
        data['${effectivePrefix}updated_at'],
      )!,
      currencySymbol: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}currency_symbol'],
      )!,
      themePreference: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}theme_preference'],
      )!,
      cachedDisplayName: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}cached_display_name'],
      ),
      cachedRole: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}cached_role'],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  $SettingsTableTable createAlias(String alias) {
    return $SettingsTableTable(attachedDatabase, alias);
  }
}

  @override

  static const String $name = 'number_update_log';
  @override
  VerificationContext validateIntegrity(
    Insertable<NumberUpdateLogTableData> instance, {
    bool isInserting = false,
  }) {
    final context = VerificationContext();
    final data = instance.toColumns(true);
    if (data.containsKey('owner_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _ownerIdMeta,
        ownerId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['owner_id']!, _ownerIdMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_ownerIdMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('synced')) {
      context.handle(
        _syncedMeta,
        synced.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['synced']!, _syncedMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('updated_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _updatedAtMeta,
        updatedAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['updated_at']!, _updatedAtMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_updatedAtMeta);

    if (data.containsKey('id')) {
      context.handle(_idMeta, id.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['id']!, _idMeta));
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_idMeta);
    }

      context.handle(
        _phoneNumberMeta,
        phoneNumber.isAcceptableOrUnknown(
          data['phone_number']!,
          _phoneNumberMeta,
        ),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_phoneNumberMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('status')) {
      context.handle(
        _statusMeta,
        status.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['status']!, _statusMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_statusMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('message')) {
      context.handle(
        _messageMeta,
        message.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['message']!, _messageMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_messageMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('created_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _createdAtMeta,
        createdAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['created_at']!, _createdAtMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_createdAtMeta);
    }
    return context;
  }

  @override

    Value<String>? id,
    Value<String>? phoneNumber,
    Value<String>? status,
    Value<String>? message,
    Value<DateTime>? createdAt,
    Value<int>? rowid,
  }) {
    return NumberUpdateLogTableCompanion(
      ownerId: ownerId ?? this.ownerId,
      synced: synced ?? this.synced,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
      id: id ?? this.id,
      phoneNumber: phoneNumber ?? this.phoneNumber,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      message: message ?? this.message,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      rowid: rowid ?? this.rowid,
    );
  }

    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    if (ownerId.present) {
      map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId.value);
    }
    if (synced.present) {
      map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced.value);
    }
    if (updatedAt.present) {
      map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt.value);
    }
    if (id.present) {
      map['id'] = Variable<String>(id.value);
    }
    if (phoneNumber.present) {
      map['phone_number'] = Variable<String>(phoneNumber.value);
    }
    if (status.present) {
      map['status'] = Variable<String>(status.value);
    }
    if (message.present) {
      map['message'] = Variable<String>(message.value);
    }
    if (createdAt.present) {
      map['created_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(createdAt.value);
    }
    if (rowid.present) {
      map['rowid'] = Variable<int>(rowid.value);
    }
    return map;
  }

    return (StringBuffer('NumberUpdateLogTableCompanion(')
          ..write('ownerId: $ownerId, ')
          ..write('synced: $synced, ')
          ..write('updatedAt: $updatedAt, ')
