  late final GeneratedColumn<String> body = GeneratedColumn<String>(
    'body',
    aliasedName,
    false,
    type: DriftSqlType.string,
    requiredDuringInsert: true,
  );
  static const VerificationMeta _relatedEntityIdMeta = const VerificationMeta(
    'relatedEntityId',
  );
  @override
  late final GeneratedColumn<String> relatedEntityId = GeneratedColumn<String>(
    'related_entity_id',
    aliasedName,
    true,
    type: DriftSqlType.string,
    requiredDuringInsert: false,
  );
  static const VerificationMeta _fireAtMeta = const VerificationMeta('fireAt');
  @override
  late final GeneratedColumn<DateTime> fireAt = GeneratedColumn<DateTime>(
    'fire_at',
    aliasedName,
    false,
    type: DriftSqlType.dateTime,
    requiredDuringInsert: true,
  );
  static const VerificationMeta _readMeta = const VerificationMeta('read');
  @override
  late final GeneratedColumn<bool> read = GeneratedColumn<bool>(
    'read',
    aliasedName,
    false,
    type: DriftSqlType.bool,
    requiredDuringInsert: false,
    defaultConstraints: GeneratedColumn.constraintIsAlways(
      'CHECK ("read" IN (0, 1))',
    ),
    defaultValue: const Constant(false),
  );
  @override
  List<GeneratedColumn> get $columns => [
    ownerId,
    synced,
    updatedAt,
    id,
    osNotificationId,
    type,
    title,
    body,
    relatedEntityId,
    fireAt,
    read,
  ];
  @override
  String get aliasedName => _alias ?? actualTableName;
  @override
  String get actualTableName => $name;
  static const String $name = 'notifications';
  @override
  VerificationContext validateIntegrity(
    Insertable<NotificationsTableData> instance, {
    bool isInserting = false,
  }) {
    final context = VerificationContext();
    final data = instance.toColumns(true);
    if (data.containsKey('owner_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _ownerIdMeta,
        ownerId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['owner_id']!, _ownerIdMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_ownerIdMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('synced')) {
      context.handle(
        _syncedMeta,
        synced.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['synced']!, _syncedMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('updated_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _updatedAtMeta,
        updatedAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['updated_at']!, _updatedAtMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_updatedAtMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('id')) {
      context.handle(_idMeta, id.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['id']!, _idMeta));
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_idMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('os_notification_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _osNotificationIdMeta,
        osNotificationId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(
          data['os_notification_id']!,
          _osNotificationIdMeta,
        ),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_osNotificationIdMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('type')) {
      context.handle(
        _typeMeta,
        type.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['type']!, _typeMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_typeMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('title')) {
      context.handle(
        _titleMeta,
        title.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['title']!, _titleMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_titleMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('body')) {
      context.handle(
        _bodyMeta,
        body.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['body']!, _bodyMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_bodyMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('related_entity_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _relatedEntityIdMeta,
        relatedEntityId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(
          data['related_entity_id']!,
          _relatedEntityIdMeta,
        ),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('fire_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _fireAtMeta,
        fireAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['fire_at']!, _fireAtMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_fireAtMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('read')) {
      context.handle(
        _readMeta,
        read.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['read']!, _readMeta),
      );
    }
    return context;
  }

  @override
  Set<GeneratedColumn> get $primaryKey => {id};
  @override
  NotificationsTableData map(Map<String, dynamic> data, {String? tablePrefix}) {
    final effectivePrefix = tablePrefix != null ? '$tablePrefix.' : '';
    return NotificationsTableData(
      ownerId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}owner_id'],
      )!,
      synced: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.bool,
        data['${effectivePrefix}synced'],
      )!,
      updatedAt: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.dateTime,
        data['${effectivePrefix}updated_at'],
      )!,
      id: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}id'],
      )!,
      osNotificationId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.int,
        data['${effectivePrefix}os_notification_id'],
      )!,
      type: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}type'],
      )!,
      title: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}title'],
      )!,
      body: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}body'],
      )!,
      relatedEntityId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}related_entity_id'],
      ),
      fireAt: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.dateTime,
        data['${effectivePrefix}fire_at'],
      )!,
      read: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.bool,
        data['${effectivePrefix}read'],
      )!,
    );
  }

  @override
  $NotificationsTableTable createAlias(String alias) {
    return $NotificationsTableTable(attachedDatabase, alias);
  }
}

class NotificationsTableData extends DataClass
    implements Insertable<NotificationsTableData> {
  final String ownerId;
  final bool synced;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  final String id;

  /// A stable, deterministic int derived from [id] (see
  /// `core/notifications/notification_id.dart`) — `flutter_local_notifications`
  /// schedules/cancels by int id, not by our own string [id].
  final int osNotificationId;

  /// One of `NotificationType`'s enum names (`core/notifications/notification_type.dart`).
  final String type;
  final String title;
  final String body;

  /// What this notification is about, if anything — lets tapping it (or
  /// cancelling it when the underlying situation resolves, e.g. a debt
  /// getting paid off) jump straight back to the right row without
  /// re-deriving it from [type]. Free-form on purpose (a customer id, a
  /// line id, a company id, ...) — the inbox UI dispatches on [type] to
  /// know how to interpret it.
  final String? relatedEntityId;
  final DateTime fireAt;
  final bool read;
  const NotificationsTableData({
    required this.ownerId,
    required this.synced,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.id,
    required this.osNotificationId,
    required this.type,
    required this.title,
    required this.body,
    this.relatedEntityId,
    required this.fireAt,
    required this.read,
  });
  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId);
    map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced);
    map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt);
    map['id'] = Variable<String>(id);
    map['os_notification_id'] = Variable<int>(osNotificationId);
    map['type'] = Variable<String>(type);
    map['title'] = Variable<String>(title);
    map['body'] = Variable<String>(body);
    if (!nullToAbsent || relatedEntityId != null) {
      map['related_entity_id'] = Variable<String>(relatedEntityId);
    }
    map['fire_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(fireAt);
    map['read'] = Variable<bool>(read);
    return map;
  }

  NotificationsTableCompanion toCompanion(bool nullToAbsent) {
    return NotificationsTableCompanion(
      ownerId: Value(ownerId),
      synced: Value(synced),
      updatedAt: Value(updatedAt),
      id: Value(id),
      osNotificationId: Value(osNotificationId),
      type: Value(type),
      title: Value(title),
      body: Value(body),
      relatedEntityId: relatedEntityId == null && nullToAbsent
          ? const Value.absent()
          : Value(relatedEntityId),
      fireAt: Value(fireAt),
      read: Value(read),
    );
  }

  factory NotificationsTableData.fromJson(
    Map<String, dynamic> json, {
    ValueSerializer? serializer,
  }) {
    serializer ??= driftRuntimeOptions.defaultSerializer;
    return NotificationsTableData(
      ownerId: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['ownerId']),
      synced: serializer.fromJson<bool>(json['synced']),
      updatedAt: serializer.fromJson<DateTime>(json['updatedAt']),
      id: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['id']),
      osNotificationId: serializer.fromJson<int>(json['osNotificationId']),
      type: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['type']),
      title: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['title']),
      body: serializer.fromJson<String>(json['body']),
      relatedEntityId: serializer.fromJson<String?>(json['relatedEntityId']),
      fireAt: serializer.fromJson<DateTime>(json['fireAt']),
      read: serializer.fromJson<bool>(json['read']),
    );
  }
  @override
  Map<String, dynamic> toJson({ValueSerializer? serializer}) {
    serializer ??= driftRuntimeOptions.defaultSerializer;
    return <String, dynamic>{
      'ownerId': serializer.toJson<String>(ownerId),
      'synced': serializer.toJson<bool>(synced),
      'updatedAt': serializer.toJson<DateTime>(updatedAt),
      'id': serializer.toJson<String>(id),
      'osNotificationId': serializer.toJson<int>(osNotificationId),
      'type': serializer.toJson<String>(type),
      'title': serializer.toJson<String>(title),
      'body': serializer.toJson<String>(body),
      'relatedEntityId': serializer.toJson<String?>(relatedEntityId),
      'fireAt': serializer.toJson<DateTime>(fireAt),
      'read': serializer.toJson<bool>(read),
    };
  }

  NotificationsTableData copyWith({
    String? ownerId,
    bool? synced,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
    String? id,
    int? osNotificationId,
    String? type,
    String? title,
    String? body,
    Value<String?> relatedEntityId = const Value.absent(),
    DateTime? fireAt,
    bool? read,
  }) => NotificationsTableData(
    ownerId: ownerId ?? this.ownerId,
    synced: synced ?? this.synced,
    updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    id: id ?? this.id,
    osNotificationId: osNotificationId ?? this.osNotificationId,
    type: type ?? this.type,
    title: title ?? this.title,
    body: body ?? this.body,
    relatedEntityId: relatedEntityId.present
        ? relatedEntityId.value
        : this.relatedEntityId,
    fireAt: fireAt ?? this.fireAt,
    read: read ?? this.read,
  );
  NotificationsTableData copyWithCompanion(NotificationsTableCompanion data) {
    return NotificationsTableData(
      ownerId: data.ownerId.present ? data.ownerId.value : this.ownerId,
      synced: data.synced.present ? data.synced.value : this.synced,
      updatedAt: data.updatedAt.present ? data.updatedAt.value : this.updatedAt,
      id: data.id.present ? data.id.value : this.id,
      osNotificationId: data.osNotificationId.present
          ? data.osNotificationId.value
          : this.osNotificationId,
      type: data.type.present ? data.type.value : this.type,
      title: data.title.present ? data.title.value : this.title,
      body: data.body.present ? data.body.value : this.body,
      relatedEntityId: data.relatedEntityId.present
          ? data.relatedEntityId.value
          : this.relatedEntityId,
      fireAt: data.fireAt.present ? data.fireAt.value : this.fireAt,
      read: data.read.present ? data.read.value : this.read,
    );
  }

  @override
  String toString() {
    return (StringBuffer('NotificationsTableData(')
          ..write('ownerId: $ownerId, ')
          ..write('synced: $synced, ')
          ..write('updatedAt: $updatedAt, ')
          ..write('id: $id, ')
          ..write('osNotificationId: $osNotificationId, ')
          ..write('type: $type, ')
          ..write('title: $title, ')
          ..write('body: $body, ')
          ..write('relatedEntityId: $relatedEntityId, ')
          ..write('fireAt: $fireAt, ')
          ..write('read: $read')
          ..write(')'))
        .toString();
  }

  @override
  int get hashCode => Object.hash(
    ownerId,
    synced,
    updatedAt,
    id,
    osNotificationId,
    type,
    title,
    body,
    relatedEntityId,
    fireAt,
    read,
  );
  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      identical(this, other) ||
      (other is NotificationsTableData &&
          other.ownerId == this.ownerId &&
          other.synced == this.synced &&
          other.updatedAt == this.updatedAt &&
          other.id == this.id &&
          other.osNotificationId == this.osNotificationId &&
          other.type == this.type &&
          other.title == this.title &&
          other.body == this.body &&
          other.relatedEntityId == this.relatedEntityId &&
          other.fireAt == this.fireAt &&
          other.read == this.read);
}

class NotificationsTableCompanion
    extends UpdateCompanion<NotificationsTableData> {
  final Value<String> ownerId;
  final Value<bool> synced;
  final Value<DateTime> updatedAt;
  final Value<String> id;
  final Value<int> osNotificationId;
  final Value<String> type;
  final Value<String> title;
  final Value<String> body;
  final Value<String?> relatedEntityId;
  final Value<DateTime> fireAt;
  final Value<bool> read;
  final Value<int> rowid;
  const NotificationsTableCompanion({
    this.ownerId = const Value.absent(),
    this.synced = const Value.absent(),
    this.updatedAt = const Value.absent(),
    this.id = const Value.absent(),
    this.osNotificationId = const Value.absent(),
    this.type = const Value.absent(),
    this.title = const Value.absent(),
    this.body = const Value.absent(),
    this.relatedEntityId = const Value.absent(),
    this.fireAt = const Value.absent(),
    this.read = const Value.absent(),
    this.rowid = const Value.absent(),
  });
  NotificationsTableCompanion.insert({
    required String ownerId,
    this.synced = const Value.absent(),
    required DateTime updatedAt,
    required String id,
    required int osNotificationId,
    required String type,
    required String title,
    required String body,
    this.relatedEntityId = const Value.absent(),
    required DateTime fireAt,
    this.read = const Value.absent(),
    this.rowid = const Value.absent(),
  }) : ownerId = Value(ownerId),
       updatedAt = Value(updatedAt),
       id = Value(id),
       osNotificationId = Value(osNotificationId),
       type = Value(type),
       title = Value(title),
       body = Value(body),
       fireAt = Value(fireAt);
  static Insertable<NotificationsTableData> custom({
    Expression<String>? ownerId,
    Expression<bool>? synced,
    Expression<DateTime>? updatedAt,
    Expression<String>? id,
    Expression<int>? osNotificationId,
    Expression<String>? type,
    Expression<String>? title,
    Expression<String>? body,
    Expression<String>? relatedEntityId,
    Expression<DateTime>? fireAt,
    Expression<bool>? read,
    Expression<int>? rowid,
  }) {
    return RawValuesInsertable({
      if (ownerId != null) 'owner_id': ownerId,
      if (synced != null) 'synced': synced,
      if (updatedAt != null) 'updated_at': updatedAt,
      if (id != null) 'id': id,
      if (osNotificationId != null) 'os_notification_id': osNotificationId,
      if (type != null) 'type': type,
      if (title != null) 'title': title,
      if (body != null) 'body': body,
      if (relatedEntityId != null) 'related_entity_id': relatedEntityId,
      if (fireAt != null) 'fire_at': fireAt,
      if (read != null) 'read': read,
      if (rowid != null) 'rowid': rowid,
    });
  }

  NotificationsTableCompanion copyWith({
    Value<String>? ownerId,
    Value<bool>? synced,
    Value<DateTime>? updatedAt,
    Value<String>? id,
    Value<int>? osNotificationId,
    Value<String>? type,
    Value<String>? title,
    Value<String>? body,
    Value<String?>? relatedEntityId,
    Value<DateTime>? fireAt,
    Value<bool>? read,
    Value<int>? rowid,
  }) {
    return NotificationsTableCompanion(
      ownerId: ownerId ?? this.ownerId,
      synced: synced ?? this.synced,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
      id: id ?? this.id,
      osNotificationId: osNotificationId ?? this.osNotificationId,
      type: type ?? this.type,
      title: title ?? this.title,
      body: body ?? this.body,
      relatedEntityId: relatedEntityId ?? this.relatedEntityId,
      fireAt: fireAt ?? this.fireAt,
      read: read ?? this.read,
      rowid: rowid ?? this.rowid,
    );
  }

  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    if (ownerId.present) {
      map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId.value);
    }
    if (synced.present) {
      map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced.value);
    }
    if (updatedAt.present) {
      map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt.value);
    }
    if (id.present) {
      map['id'] = Variable<String>(id.value);
    }
    if (osNotificationId.present) {
      map['os_notification_id'] = Variable<int>(osNotificationId.value);
    }
    if (type.present) {
      map['type'] = Variable<String>(type.value);
    }
    if (title.present) {
      map['title'] = Variable<String>(title.value);
    }
    if (body.present) {
      map['body'] = Variable<String>(body.value);
    }
    if (relatedEntityId.present) {
      map['related_entity_id'] = Variable<String>(relatedEntityId.value);
    }
    if (fireAt.present) {
      map['fire_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(fireAt.value);
    }
    if (read.present) {
      map['read'] = Variable<bool>(read.value);
    }
    if (rowid.present) {
      map['rowid'] = Variable<int>(rowid.value);
    }
    return map;
  }

  @override
  String toString() {
    return (StringBuffer('NotificationsTableCompanion(')
          ..write('ownerId: $ownerId, ')
          ..write('synced: $synced, ')
          ..write('updatedAt: $updatedAt, ')
          ..write('id: $id, ')
          ..write('osNotificationId: $osNotificationId, ')
          ..write('type: $type, ')
          ..write('title: $title, ')
          ..write('body: $body, ')
          ..write('relatedEntityId: $relatedEntityId, ')
          ..write('fireAt: $fireAt, ')
          ..write('read: $read, ')
          ..write('rowid: $rowid')
          ..write(')'))
        .toString();
  }
}

    companyId,

    amountCharged,
    gbAdded,
    collected,

  ];
  @override
  String get aliasedName => _alias ?? actualTableName;
  @override
  String get actualTableName => $name;
  static const String $name = 'additional_services';
  @override
  VerificationContext validateIntegrity(
    Insertable<AdditionalServicesTableData> instance, {
    bool isInserting = false,
  }) {
    final context = VerificationContext();
    final data = instance.toColumns(true);
    if (data.containsKey('owner_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _ownerIdMeta,
        ownerId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['owner_id']!, _ownerIdMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_ownerIdMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('synced')) {
      context.handle(
        _syncedMeta,
        synced.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['synced']!, _syncedMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('updated_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _updatedAtMeta,
        updatedAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['updated_at']!, _updatedAtMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_updatedAtMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('id')) {
      context.handle(_idMeta, id.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['id']!, _idMeta));
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_idMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('line_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _lineIdMeta,
        lineId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['line_id']!, _lineIdMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_lineIdMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('customer_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _customerIdMeta,
        customerId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['customer_id']!, _customerIdMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_customerIdMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('company_id')) {
      context.handle(
        _companyIdMeta,
        companyId.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['company_id']!, _companyIdMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('amount_deducted')) {
      context.handle(
        _amountDeductedMeta,
        amountDeducted.isAcceptableOrUnknown(
          data['amount_deducted']!,
          _amountDeductedMeta,
        ),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_amountDeductedMeta);
    }
    if (data.containsKey('amount_charged')) {
      context.handle(
        _amountChargedMeta,
        amountCharged.isAcceptableOrUnknown(
          data['amount_charged']!,
          _amountChargedMeta,
        ),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('gb_added')) {
      context.handle(
        _gbAddedMeta,
        gbAdded.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['gb_added']!, _gbAddedMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('collected')) {
      context.handle(
        _collectedMeta,
        collected.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['collected']!, _collectedMeta),
      );
    }
    if (data.containsKey('created_at')) {
      context.handle(
        _createdAtMeta,
        createdAt.isAcceptableOrUnknown(data['created_at']!, _createdAtMeta),
      );
    } else if (isInserting) {
      context.missing(_createdAtMeta);
    }
    return context;
  }

  @override
  Set<GeneratedColumn> get $primaryKey => {id};
  @override
  AdditionalServicesTableData map(
    Map<String, dynamic> data, {
    String? tablePrefix,
  }) {
    final effectivePrefix = tablePrefix != null ? '$tablePrefix.' : '';
    return AdditionalServicesTableData(
      ownerId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}owner_id'],
      )!,
      synced: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.bool,
        data['${effectivePrefix}synced'],
      )!,
      updatedAt: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.dateTime,
        data['${effectivePrefix}updated_at'],
      )!,
      id: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}id'],
      )!,
      lineId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}line_id'],
      )!,
      customerId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}customer_id'],
      )!,
      companyId: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.string,
        data['${effectivePrefix}company_id'],
      ),
      amountDeducted: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.double,
        data['${effectivePrefix}amount_deducted'],
      )!,
      amountCharged: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.double,
        data['${effectivePrefix}amount_charged'],
      )!,
      gbAdded: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.double,
        data['${effectivePrefix}gb_added'],
      )!,
      collected: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.bool,
        data['${effectivePrefix}collected'],
      )!,
      createdAt: attachedDatabase.typeMapping.read(
        DriftSqlType.dateTime,
        data['${effectivePrefix}created_at'],
      )!,
    );
  }

  @override
  $AdditionalServicesTableTable createAlias(String alias) {
    return $AdditionalServicesTableTable(attachedDatabase, alias);
  }
}

class AdditionalServicesTableData extends DataClass
    implements Insertable<AdditionalServicesTableData> {
  final String ownerId;
  final bool synced;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  final String id;
  final String lineId;
  final String customerId;
  final String? companyId;
  final double amountDeducted;
  final double amountCharged;
  final double gbAdded;

  /// Whether the seller has collected [amountCharged] from the customer
  /// yet — flipped by its own dedicated "Mark collected" action, never by
  /// the Line's own "Add payment" flow (see class doc comment on why
  /// these stay independent).
  final bool collected;

  /// The actual moment this top-up was activated — kept separate from
  /// [TenantColumns.updatedAt] (which changes again when [collected]
  /// flips) so the per-line history list has a stable, real creation
  /// date to sort/display by.
  final DateTime createdAt;
  const AdditionalServicesTableData({
    required this.ownerId,
    required this.synced,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.id,
    required this.lineId,
    required this.customerId,
    this.companyId,
    required this.amountDeducted,
    required this.amountCharged,
    required this.gbAdded,
    required this.collected,
    required this.createdAt,
  });
  @override
  Map<String, Expression> toColumns(bool nullToAbsent) {
    final map = <String, Expression>{};
    map['owner_id'] = Variable<String>(ownerId);
    map['synced'] = Variable<bool>(synced);
    map['updated_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(updatedAt);
    map['id'] = Variable<String>(id);
    map['line_id'] = Variable<String>(lineId);
    map['customer_id'] = Variable<String>(customerId);
    if (!nullToAbsent || companyId != null) {
      map['company_id'] = Variable<String>(companyId);
    }
    map['amount_deducted'] = Variable<double>(amountDeducted);
    map['amount_charged'] = Variable<double>(amountCharged);
    map['gb_added'] = Variable<double>(gbAdded);
    map['collected'] = Variable<bool>(collected);
    map['created_at'] = Variable<DateTime>(createdAt);
    return map;
  }

  AdditionalServicesTableCompanion toCompanion(bool nullToAbsent) {
    return AdditionalServicesTableCompanion(
      ownerId: Value(ownerId),
      synced: Value(synced),
      updatedAt: Value(updatedAt),
      id: Value(id),
      lineId: Value(lineId),
      customerId: Value(customerId),
      companyId: companyId == null && nullToAbsent
          ? const Value.absent()
          : Value(companyId),
      amountDeducted: Value(amountDeducted),
      amountCharged: Value(amountCharged),
      gbAdded: Value(gbAdded),
      collected: Value(collected),
      createdAt: Value(createdAt),
    );
  }

    super.$_typedResult,
  );

  static $CompanyCatalogTableTable _companyCatalogIdTable(_$AppDatabase db) =>
      db.companyCatalogTable.createAlias(
        'package_catalog__company_catalog_id__company_catalog__id',
      );

  $$CompanyCatalogTableTableProcessedTableManager get companyCatalogId {
    final $_column = $_itemColumn<String>('company_catalog_id')!;

    final manager = $$CompanyCatalogTableTableTableManager(
      $_db,
      $_db.companyCatalogTable,
    ).filter((f) => f.id.sqlEquals($_column));
    final item = $_typedResult.readTableOrNull(_companyCatalogIdTable($_db));
    if (item == null) return manager;
    return ProcessedTableManager(
      manager.$state.copyWith(prefetchedData: [item]),
    );
  }
}

class $$CompaniesTableTableTableManager
    extends
        RootTableManager<
          _$AppDatabase,
          $CompaniesTableTable,
          CompaniesTableData,
          $$CompaniesTableTableFilterComposer,
          $$CompaniesTableTableOrderingComposer,
          $$CompaniesTableTableAnnotationComposer,
          $$CompaniesTableTableCreateCompanionBuilder,
          $$CompaniesTableTableUpdateCompanionBuilder,
          (CompaniesTableData, $$CompaniesTableTableReferences),
          CompaniesTableData,
          PrefetchHooks Function({
            bool catalogCompanyId,
            bool packagesTableRefs,
            bool shipmentsTableRefs,
            bool linesTableRefs,
            bool operationsTableRefs,
          })
        > {
  $$CompaniesTableTableTableManager(
    _$AppDatabase db,
    $CompaniesTableTable table,
  ) : super(
        TableManagerState(
          db: db,
          table: table,
          createFilteringComposer: () =>
              $$CompaniesTableTableFilterComposer($db: db, $table: table),
          createOrderingComposer: () =>
              $$CompaniesTableTableOrderingComposer($db: db, $table: table),
          createComputedFieldComposer: () =>
              $$CompaniesTableTableAnnotationComposer($db: db, $table: table),
          updateCompanionCallback:
              ({
                Value<String> ownerId = const Value.absent(),
                Value<bool> synced = const Value.absent(),
                Value<DateTime> updatedAt = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String> id = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String> name = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> logoUrl = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> notes = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String> status = const Value.absent(),
                Value<bool> pinned = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> catalogCompanyId = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> numberUpdateProvider = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> numberUpdateUrl = const Value.absent(),
                Value<bool> allowNegativeBalance = const Value.absent(),
                Value<int> rowid = const Value.absent(),
              }) => CompaniesTableCompanion(
                ownerId: ownerId,
                synced: synced,
                updatedAt: updatedAt,
                id: id,
                name: name,
                logoUrl: logoUrl,
                notes: notes,
                status: status,
                pinned: pinned,
                catalogCompanyId: catalogCompanyId,
                numberUpdateProvider: numberUpdateProvider,
                numberUpdateUrl: numberUpdateUrl,
                allowNegativeBalance: allowNegativeBalance,
                rowid: rowid,
              ),
          createCompanionCallback:
              ({
                required String ownerId,
                Value<bool> synced = const Value.absent(),
                required DateTime updatedAt,
                required String id,
                required String name,
                Value<String?> logoUrl = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> notes = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String> status = const Value.absent(),
                Value<bool> pinned = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> catalogCompanyId = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> numberUpdateProvider = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> numberUpdateUrl = const Value.absent(),
                Value<bool> allowNegativeBalance = const Value.absent(),
                Value<int> rowid = const Value.absent(),
              }) => CompaniesTableCompanion.insert(
                ownerId: ownerId,
                synced: synced,
                updatedAt: updatedAt,
                id: id,
                name: name,
                logoUrl: logoUrl,
                notes: notes,
                status: status,
                pinned: pinned,
                catalogCompanyId: catalogCompanyId,
                numberUpdateProvider: numberUpdateProvider,
                numberUpdateUrl: numberUpdateUrl,
                allowNegativeBalance: allowNegativeBalance,
                rowid: rowid,
              ),
          withReferenceMapper: (p0) => p0
              .map(
                (e) => (
                  e.readTable(table),
                  $$CompaniesTableTableReferences(db, table, e),
                ),
              )
              .toList(),
          prefetchHooksCallback:
              ({
                catalogCompanyId = false,
                packagesTableRefs = false,
                shipmentsTableRefs = false,
                linesTableRefs = false,
                operationsTableRefs = false,
              }) {
                return PrefetchHooks(
                  db: db,
                  explicitlyWatchedTables: [
                    if (packagesTableRefs) db.packagesTable,
                    if (shipmentsTableRefs) db.shipmentsTable,
                    if (linesTableRefs) db.linesTable,
                    if (operationsTableRefs) db.operationsTable,
                  ],
                  addJoins:
                      <
                        T extends TableManagerState<
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic,
                          dynamic
                        >
                      >(state) {
                        if (catalogCompanyId) {
                          state =
                              state.withJoin(
                                    currentTable: table,
                                    currentColumn: table.catalogCompanyId,
                                    referencedTable:
                                        $$CompaniesTableTableReferences
                                            ._catalogCompanyIdTable(db),
                                    referencedColumn:
                                        $$CompaniesTableTableReferences
                                            ._catalogCompanyIdTable(db)
                                            .id,
                                  )
                                  as T;
                        }

                        return state;
                      },
                  getPrefetchedDataCallback: (items) async {
                    return [
                      if (packagesTableRefs)
                        await $_getPrefetchedData<
                          CompaniesTableData,
                          $CompaniesTableTable,
                          PackagesTableData
                        >(
                          currentTable: table,
                          referencedTable: $$CompaniesTableTableReferences
                              ._packagesTableRefsTable(db),
                          managerFromTypedResult: (p0) =>
                              $$CompaniesTableTableReferences(
                                db,
                                table,
                                p0,
                              ).packagesTableRefs,
                          referencedItemsForCurrentItem:
                              (item, referencedItems) => referencedItems.where(
                                (e) => e.companyId == item.id,
                              ),
                          typedResults: items,
                        ),
                      if (shipmentsTableRefs)
                        await $_getPrefetchedData<
                          CompaniesTableData,
                          $CompaniesTableTable,
                          ShipmentsTableData
                        >(
                          currentTable: table,
                          referencedTable: $$CompaniesTableTableReferences
                              ._shipmentsTableRefsTable(db),
                          managerFromTypedResult: (p0) =>
                              $$CompaniesTableTableReferences(
                                db,
                                table,
                                p0,
                              ).shipmentsTableRefs,
                          referencedItemsForCurrentItem:
                              (item, referencedItems) => referencedItems.where(
                                (e) => e.companyId == item.id,
                              ),
                          typedResults: items,
                        ),
                      if (linesTableRefs)
                        await $_getPrefetchedData<
                          CompaniesTableData,
                          $CompaniesTableTable,
                          LinesTableData
                        >(
                          currentTable: table,
                          referencedTable: $$CompaniesTableTableReferences
                              ._linesTableRefsTable(db),
                          managerFromTypedResult: (p0) =>
                              $$CompaniesTableTableReferences(
                                db,
                                table,
                                p0,
                              ).linesTableRefs,
                          referencedItemsForCurrentItem:
                              (item, referencedItems) => referencedItems.where(
                                (e) => e.companyId == item.id,
                              ),
                          typedResults: items,
                        ),
                      if (operationsTableRefs)
                        await $_getPrefetchedData<
                          CompaniesTableData,
                          $CompaniesTableTable,
                          OperationsTableData
                        >(
                          currentTable: table,
                          referencedTable: $$CompaniesTableTableReferences
                              ._operationsTableRefsTable(db),
                          managerFromTypedResult: (p0) =>
                              $$CompaniesTableTableReferences(
                                db,
                                table,
                                p0,
                              ).operationsTableRefs,

  $$PackagesTableTableReferences(
    super.$_db,
    super.$_table,
    super.$_typedResult,
  );

  static $CompaniesTableTable _companyIdTable(_$AppDatabase db) =>
      db.companiesTable.createAlias('packages__company_id__companies__id');

  $$CompaniesTableTableProcessedTableManager get companyId {
    final $_column = $_itemColumn<String>('company_id')!;

    final manager = $$CompaniesTableTableTableManager(
      $_db,
      $_db.companiesTable,
    ).filter((f) => f.id.sqlEquals($_column));
    final item = $_typedResult.readTableOrNull(_companyIdTable($_db));
    if (item == null) return manager;
    return ProcessedTableManager(
      manager.$state.copyWith(prefetchedData: [item]),
    );
  }

  static MultiTypedResultKey<$LinesTableTable, List<LinesTableData>>
  _linesTableRefsTable(_$AppDatabase db) => MultiTypedResultKey.fromTable(
    db.linesTable,
    aliasName: 'packages__id__lines__package_id',
  );

  $$LinesTableTableProcessedTableManager get linesTableRefs {

      $_db,
      $_db.linesTable,

class $$PackagesTableTableTableManager
    extends
        RootTableManager<
          _$AppDatabase,
          $PackagesTableTable,
          PackagesTableData,
          $$PackagesTableTableFilterComposer,
          $$PackagesTableTableOrderingComposer,
          $$PackagesTableTableAnnotationComposer,
          $$PackagesTableTableCreateCompanionBuilder,
          $$PackagesTableTableUpdateCompanionBuilder,
          (PackagesTableData, $$PackagesTableTableReferences),
          PackagesTableData,
          PrefetchHooks Function({bool companyId, bool linesTableRefs})
        > {
  $$PackagesTableTableTableManager(_$AppDatabase db, $PackagesTableTable table)
    : super(
        TableManagerState(
          db: db,
          table: table,
          createFilteringComposer: () =>
              $$PackagesTableTableFilterComposer($db: db, $table: table),
          createOrderingComposer: () =>
              $$PackagesTableTableOrderingComposer($db: db, $table: table),
          createComputedFieldComposer: () =>
              $$PackagesTableTableAnnotationComposer($db: db, $table: table),
          updateCompanionCallback:
              ({
                Value<String> ownerId = const Value.absent(),
                Value<bool> synced = const Value.absent(),
                Value<DateTime> updatedAt = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String> id = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String> companyId = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String> name = const Value.absent(),
                Value<String?> simType = const Value.absent(),
                Value<double> wholesaleCost = const Value.absent(),
                Value<double> retailPrice = const Value.absent(),
                Value<int> rowid = const Value.absent(),
              }) => PackagesTableCompanion(
                ownerId: ownerId,
                synced: synced,
                updatedAt: updatedAt,
                id: id,
                companyId: companyId,
                name: name,
                simType: simType,
                wholesaleCost: wholesaleCost,
                retailPrice: retailPrice,
                rowid: rowid,
              ),
          createCompanionCallback:
              ({
                required String ownerId,
                Value<bool> synced = const Value.absent(),
                required DateTime updatedAt,
                required String id,
                required String companyId,
                required String name,
                Value<String?> simType = const Value.absent(),
                Value<double> wholesaleCost = const Value.absent(),
                Value<double> retailPrice = const Value.absent(),
                Value<int> rowid = const Value.absent(),
              }) => PackagesTableCompanion.insert(
                ownerId: ownerId,
                synced: synced,
                updatedAt: updatedAt,
                id: id,
                companyId: companyId,
                name: name,
                simType: simType,
                wholesaleCost: wholesaleCost,
                retailPrice: retailPrice,
                rowid: rowid,
              ),
          withReferenceMapper: (p0) => p0
              .map(
                (e) => (
                  e.readTable(table),
                  $$PackagesTableTableReferences(db, table, e),
                ),
              )
              .toList(),
          prefetchHooksCallback: ({companyId = false, linesTableRefs = false}) {
            return PrefetchHooks(
              db: db,
              explicitlyWatchedTables: [if (linesTableRefs) db.linesTable],
              addJoins:
                  <
                    T extends TableManagerState<
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic,
                      dynamic
                    >
                  >(state) {
                    if (companyId) {
                      state =
                          state.withJoin(
                                currentTable: table,
                                currentColumn: table.companyId,
                                referencedTable: $$PackagesTableTableReferences
                                    ._companyIdTable(db),
                                referencedColumn: $$PackagesTableTableReferences
                                    ._companyIdTable(db)
                                    .id,
                              )
                              as T;
                    }

                    return state;
                  },
              getPrefetchedDataCallback: (items) async {
                return [
                  if (linesTableRefs)
                    await $_getPrefetchedData<
                      PackagesTableData,
                      $PackagesTableTable,
                      LinesTableData
                    >(
                      currentTable: table,
                      referencedTable: $$PackagesTableTableReferences
                          ._linesTableRefsTable(db),
                      managerFromTypedResult: (p0) =>
                          $$PackagesTableTableReferences(
                            db,
                            table,
                            p0,
                          ).linesTableRefs,
                      referencedItemsForCurrentItem: (item, referencedItems) =>
                          referencedItems.where((e) => e.packageId == item.id),
                      typedResults: items,
                    ),
                ];
              },
            );
          },
        ),
      );
}

      manager.$state.copyWith(prefetchedData: [item]),
    );
  }

  static MultiTypedResultKey<$OperationsTableTable, List<OperationsTableData>>
  _operationsTableRefsTable(_$AppDatabase db) => MultiTypedResultKey.fromTable(
    db.operationsTable,
    aliasName: 'lines__id__operations__line_id',
  );

  $$OperationsTableTableProcessedTableManager get operationsTableRefs {
    final manager = $$OperationsTableTableTableManager(
      $_db,
      $_db.operationsTable,
    ).filter((f) => f.lineId.id.sqlEquals($_itemColumn<String>('id')!));

    final cache = $_typedResult.readTableOrNull(
      _operationsTableRefsTable($_db),
    );
    return ProcessedTableManager(
      manager.$state.copyWith(prefetchedData: cache),
    );
  }
}

          prefetchHooksCallback:
              ({

                companyId = false,
                packageId = false,
                operationsTableRefs = false,

                return PrefetchHooks(
                  db: db,
                  explicitlyWatchedTables: [
                    if (operationsTableRefs) db.operationsTable,

                  addJoins:
                      <
                        T extends TableManagerState<

                                        $$LinesTableTableReferences
                                            ._companyIdTable(db)
                                            .id,

                                  as T;
                        }
                        if (packageId) {
                          state =
                              state.withJoin(
                                    currentTable: table,
                                    currentColumn: table.packageId,
                                    referencedTable: $$LinesTableTableReferences
                                        ._packageIdTable(db),
                                    referencedColumn:
                                        $$LinesTableTableReferences
                                            ._packageIdTable(db)
                                            .id,
                                  )
                                  as T;
                        }

                        return state;
                      },
                  getPrefetchedDataCallback: (items) async {
                    return [
                      if (operationsTableRefs)
                        await $_getPrefetchedData<
                          LinesTableData,
                          $LinesTableTable,
                          OperationsTableData
                        >(
                          currentTable: table,
                          referencedTable: $$LinesTableTableReferences
                              ._operationsTableRefsTable(db),
                          managerFromTypedResult: (p0) =>
                              $$LinesTableTableReferences(
                                db,
                                table,
                                p0,
                              ).operationsTableRefs,
                          referencedItemsForCurrentItem:
                              (item, referencedItems) => referencedItems.where(
                                (e) => e.lineId == item.id,
                              ),
                          typedResults: items,
                        ),
                    ];
                  },
                );
              },
        ),
      );
}

===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\database\tables\additional_services_table.dart =====
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

import 'tenant_columns.dart';

/// One row per "Additional Service" top-up applied to a Line — a seller
/// activating extra data (fixed 30GB by default, editable) from the
/// supplying company when a customer's line runs out before month's end.
/// Deliberately its own table rather than new columns on `LinesTable`:
/// [amountDeducted] (what it costs against the *company's* balance) and
/// [amountCharged] (what, if anything, the seller passes on to the
/// *customer* as debt) are both independent of the Line's own
/// `retailPrice`/`wholesaleCost`/`amountRemaining` — folding them in
/// there would corrupt `Line.applyPayment`'s `retailPrice - amountPaid`
/// invariant the moment any future payment recomputed it. Keeping this
/// separate also gives a real per-line history (one row per top-up)
/// instead of a single mutated running total.
///
/// [companyId] is a snapshot of whichever company the deduction actually
/// came from at creation time — deliberately never touched again even if
/// the Line itself is later reassigned to a different company (see
/// `CustomersCubit.updateLine`'s doc comment on why that reassignment
/// must not retroactively move a historical deduction).
///
/// [customerId] is denormalized (same reasoning as `LinesTable.customerId`
/// itself) so `CustomersState.totalOwedFor` can sum unpaid
/// [amountCharged] without joining through `lines`.
class AdditionalServicesTable extends Table with TenantColumns {
  @override
  String get tableName => 'additional_services';

  TextColumn get id => text()();
  TextColumn get lineId => text()();
  TextColumn get customerId => text()();
  TextColumn get companyId => text().nullable()();

  RealColumn get amountDeducted => real()();
  RealColumn get amountCharged => real().withDefault(const Constant(0))();
  RealColumn get gbAdded => real().withDefault(const Constant(30))();

  /// Whether the seller has collected [amountCharged] from the customer
  /// yet — flipped by its own dedicated "Mark collected" action, never by
  /// the Line's own "Add payment" flow (see class doc comment on why
  /// these stay independent).
  BoolColumn get collected => boolean().withDefault(const Constant(false))();

  /// The actual moment this top-up was activated — kept separate from
  /// [TenantColumns.updatedAt] (which changes again when [collected]
  /// flips) so the per-line history list has a stable, real creation
  /// date to sort/display by.
  DateTimeColumn get createdAt => dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column> get primaryKey => {id};
}

/// A seller's own company record — name/logo/notes/packages are fully
/// theirs to add and edit, unlike a shared catalog entry. [catalogCompanyId]

/// replaced the earlier `seller_companies` link-table design.
class CompaniesTable extends Table with TenantColumns {

  String get tableName => 'companies';

  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get notes => text().nullable()();

===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\database\tables\lines_table.dart =====
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

import 'companies_table.dart';
import 'customers_table.dart';
import 'packages_table.dart';
import 'tenant_columns.dart';

class LinesTable extends Table with TenantColumns {
  @override
  String get tableName => 'lines';

  TextColumn get id => text()();
  TextColumn get customerId => text().references(CustomersTable, #id)();
  // Nullable: a permanently-deleted company detaches from its historical
  // Lines instead of taking them with it (a real "sold history vanished"
  // bug found in testing — Postgres cascade-deleted Lines along with the
  // company; now `on delete set null` server-side instead, see
  // supabase/migrations/0012_lines_company_set_null.sql). The
  // `companyName`/`companyInitials` snapshot columns below are exactly
  // what keeps a detached Line still displaying its (now historical)
  // company correctly.
  TextColumn get companyId => text().nullable().references(CompaniesTable, #id)();
  TextColumn get packageId => text().references(PackagesTable, #id).nullable()();
  TextColumn get simNumber => text()();
  // Historical snapshot of the company/package names at sale time —
  // stored here (not just live-joined from the current Companies list)
  // so a Line keeps displaying correctly even if the company hasn't
  // finished loading yet on this device (a real race seen in practice:
  // Lines and Companies sync independently, so a just-added company's
  // own pull can still be in flight when a Line referencing it loads),
  // is later renamed, or is archived. Nullable so pre-migration rows
  // fall back to the old live-join in `CustomersCubit._lineFromRow`.
  TextColumn get companyName => text().nullable()();
  TextColumn get companyInitials => text().nullable()();
  TextColumn get packageName => text().nullable()();
  TextColumn get packageSimType => text().nullable()();
  DateTimeColumn get purchaseDate => dateTime()();
  DateTimeColumn get expiryDate => dateTime()();
  RealColumn get wholesaleCost => real().withDefault(const Constant(0))();
  RealColumn get retailPrice => real().withDefault(const Constant(0))();
  RealColumn get profit => real().withDefault(const Constant(0))();
  TextColumn get paymentStatus => text().withDefault(const Constant('unpaid'))();
  RealColumn get amountPaid => real().withDefault(const Constant(0))();
  RealColumn get amountRemaining => real().withDefault(const Constant(0))();

  @override
  Set<Column> get primaryKey => {id};
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\database\tables\notifications_table.dart =====
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

import 'tenant_columns.dart';

/// One row per notification — the single source of truth for both the
/// in-app "Notifications" inbox (every row, past and future, newest
/// first) and for what's currently scheduled at the OS level: whenever
/// [fireAt] is still in the future, `NotificationsCubit` has (or, on the
/// next app open, re-arms) a matching `flutter_local_notifications`
/// alarm for [osNotificationId] — Android's own AlarmManager delivers it
/// even if the app isn't running. Backed up to Supabase like every other
/// tenant table (`SyncEngine.pushPendingNotifications`/`pullNotifications`,
/// `supabase/migrations/0015_notifications.sql`) — clearing the app's
/// local storage (or reinstalling) wipes both this table and every OS
/// alarm it had armed, so without a remote copy a still-future reminder
/// would just be gone; syncing it back down on the next sign-in lets
/// `NotificationsCubit` re-arm it. [osNotificationId] is a pure function
/// of [id] (see `notification_id.dart`), so it comes back identical
/// after a reinstall and re-scheduling by it is safe.
class NotificationsTable extends Table with TenantColumns {
  @override
  String get tableName => 'notifications';

  TextColumn get id => text()();

  /// A stable, deterministic int derived from [id] (see
  /// `core/notifications/notification_id.dart`) — `flutter_local_notifications`
  /// schedules/cancels by int id, not by our own string [id].
  IntColumn get osNotificationId => integer()();

  /// One of `NotificationType`'s enum names (`core/notifications/notification_type.dart`).
  TextColumn get type => text()();
  TextColumn get title => text()();
  TextColumn get body => text()();

  /// What this notification is about, if anything — lets tapping it (or
  /// cancelling it when the underlying situation resolves, e.g. a debt
  /// getting paid off) jump straight back to the right row without
  /// re-deriving it from [type]. Free-form on purpose (a customer id, a
  /// line id, a company id, ...) — the inbox UI dispatches on [type] to
  /// know how to interpret it.
  TextColumn get relatedEntityId => text().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get fireAt => dateTime()();
  BoolColumn get read => boolean().withDefault(const Constant(false))();

  @override
  Set<Column> get primaryKey => {id};
}

/// Remembers which supplier a phone number matched last time Number
/// Update tried it, so the next update for that same number tries that
/// supplier first instead of re-running the full trial-and-error order
/// (Liyan -> QuickLine -> Areen -> Sky) every time. One row per
/// (owner, phone number) — overwritten whenever a fresh trial finds a
/// (possibly different) match.
class NumberUpdateProviderCacheTable extends Table with TenantColumns {
  @override
  String get tableName => 'number_update_provider_cache';

  TextColumn get phoneNumber => text()();
  TextColumn get provider => text()();

  @override
  Set<Column> get primaryKey => {ownerId, phoneNumber};
}

===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\database\tables\packages_table.dart =====
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

import 'companies_table.dart';
import 'tenant_columns.dart';

/// A package/bundle under one of the seller's own companies — fully
/// theirs, independent of `package_catalog` once created.
class PackagesTable extends Table with TenantColumns {
  @override
  String get tableName => 'packages';

  TextColumn get id => text()();
  TextColumn get companyId => text().references(CompaniesTable, #id)();
  TextColumn get name => text()();
  // Which SIM/line this bundle runs on (e.g. "Cellcom Bundle — monthly")
  // — see CompanyPackage's doc comment. Nullable since packages saved
  // before this field existed don't have one.
  TextColumn get simType => text().nullable()();
  RealColumn get wholesaleCost => real().withDefault(const Constant(0))();
  RealColumn get retailPrice => real().withDefault(const Constant(0))();

  @override
  Set<Column> get primaryKey => {id};
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\database\tables\pending_deletes_table.dart =====
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

/// A tombstone for a row permanently deleted locally whose matching
/// remote delete hasn't been confirmed yet. Without this, a permanent
/// delete's remote call is fire-and-forget with nothing tracking whether
/// it actually reached the server — if it hadn't (offline, or the app
/// closed before it finished), the next pull for that table re-fetches
/// the row (it's still sitting on the server) and silently resurrects
/// it locally. A row here blocks that: `SyncEngine`'s pull for
/// [entityTable] skips any id listed here, and a dedicated push step
/// keeps retrying the real remote delete until it succeeds, only then
/// removing the tombstone.
class PendingDeletesTable extends Table {
  @override
  String get tableName => 'pending_deletes';

  TextColumn get entityTable => text()();
  TextColumn get entityId => text()();
  TextColumn get ownerId => text()();

  @override
  Set<Column> get primaryKey => {entityTable, entityId};
}

import 'tenant_columns.dart';

/// One row per seller — `ownerId` (from [TenantColumns]) is the primary
/// key itself rather than a separate `id`, since there's exactly one
/// settings row per account.
class SettingsTable extends Table with TenantColumns {
  @override
  String get tableName => 'settings';

  TextColumn get currencySymbol => text().withDefault(const Constant('₪'))();
  TextColumn get themePreference => text().withDefault(const Constant('system'))();
  // A device-local cache of `profiles.display_name`, refreshed whenever
  // `ProfileCubit` fetches it successfully — purely local, never pushed
  // to or pulled from Supabase (see sync_engine.dart's settings mapping,
  // which deliberately excludes it), so it can't conflict with the real
  // source of truth. Read only as a cold-start-while-offline fallback,
  // so "Welcome back" shows the seller's actual last-known name instead
  // of their email when there's no time to fetch it fresh.
  TextColumn get cachedDisplayName => text().nullable()();

  // cold-start-while-offline fallback so a returning admin still lands
  // on the Admin Hub instead of silently falling back to the seller
  // shell just because there was no connectivity to re-confirm the role.
  TextColumn get cachedRole => text().nullable()();

  @override
  Set<Column> get primaryKey => {ownerId};
}

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

/// Shared columns every tenant-scoped table carries: who owns the row
/// (mirrors Supabase's `owner_id`, scoped by RLS server-side) and the
/// offline-first sync bookkeeping from CLAUDE.md — `synced = false`
/// means this row has local changes not yet pushed, `updatedAt` drives

import 'package:get_it/get_it.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

import '../../features/companies/presentation/cubit/companies_cubit.dart';
import '../../features/customers/presentation/cubit/customers_cubit.dart';
import '../../features/home/presentation/cubit/home_cubit.dart';
import '../../features/profile/presentation/cubit/profile_cubit.dart';
import '../activity_log/activity_logger.dart';
import '../auth/auth_service.dart';
import '../catalog/company_catalog.dart';
import '../catalog/customer_catalog.dart';
import '../config/env.dart';
import '../database/app_database.dart';
import '../notifications/notifications_cubit.dart';
import '../settings/settings_cubit.dart';
import '../sync/bloc/sync_bloc.dart';
import '../sync/connectivity_service.dart';
import '../sync/sync_engine.dart';
import '../update/update_cubit.dart';

/// Composition root: this is the one place in `core/` allowed to know
/// about concrete feature Cubits — it wires them up as app-lifetime
/// singletons so state survives navigating between tabs, and exposes the
/// narrow `CompanyCatalog`/`CustomerCatalog` interfaces (same instances)
/// for features that only need read/write access, not the full Cubit API.
final getIt = GetIt.instance;

void setupServiceLocator() {
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<AppDatabase>(() => AppDatabase());
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<ConnectivityService>(() => ConnectivityService());

  // `SyncEngine` needs a live Supabase session, so it's only registered
  // once `Env.isConfigured` — until real project credentials are supplied
  // (see core/config/env.dart), the app runs fully offline against Drift
  // and `SyncBloc` just reports `offline` forever, never null-checking.
  if (Env.isConfigured) {
    getIt.registerLazySingleton<SyncEngine>(() => SyncEngine(Supabase.instance.client, getIt<AppDatabase>()));
    getIt.registerLazySingleton<AuthService>(() => AuthService(Supabase.instance.client));
  }
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<SyncBloc>(
    () => SyncBloc(
      connectivity: getIt<ConnectivityService>(),
      engine: Env.isConfigured ? getIt<SyncEngine>() : null,
      auth: Env.isConfigured ? getIt<AuthService>() : null,
    ),
  );

  getIt.registerLazySingleton<ActivityLogger>(() => ActivityLogger());
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<UpdateCubit>(() => UpdateCubit());
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<NotificationsCubit>(() => NotificationsCubit());

  getIt.registerLazySingleton<CompaniesCubit>(() => CompaniesCubit());
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<CustomersCubit>(() => CustomersCubit());
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<ProfileCubit>(() => ProfileCubit());
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<SettingsCubit>(() => SettingsCubit());

  getIt.registerLazySingleton<CompanyCatalog>(() => getIt<CompaniesCubit>());
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<CustomerCatalog>(() => getIt<CustomersCubit>());

  // Reads through the catalog interfaces above, same as Profits/Reports —
  // registered after them so `getIt<CompanyCatalog>()`/`getIt<CustomerCatalog>()`
  // resolve to the same instances those pages already share.
  getIt.registerLazySingleton<HomeCubit>(
    () => HomeCubit(
      customerCatalog: getIt<CustomerCatalog>(),
      companyCatalog: getIt<CompanyCatalog>(),
      profileCubit: getIt<ProfileCubit>(),
    ),
  );
}
===== D:\Android\sim_sales_manager\lib\core\navigation\logout.dart =====
import 'package:easy_localization/easy_localization.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';

import '../auth/auth_service.dart';
import '../config/env.dart';
import '../di/service_locator.dart';
import '../widgets/confirm_dialog.dart';
import 'route_names.dart';

/// Shared logout confirmation, used by both More's Logout row and
/// Settings' Logout button (APP_CONTEXT.md: they should behave the
/// same). Clears the whole nav stack down to Login.
void confirmLogout(BuildContext context) {
  showConfirmDialog(
    context,
    title: 'logout.title'.tr(),
    message: 'logout.message'.tr(),
    confirmLabel: 'logout.confirm'.tr(),
    onConfirm: () async {
      // Must actually clear the Supabase session, not just navigate away
      // — otherwise `main()`'s "already signed in? skip Login" check
      // would just bounce the user right back in on next launch.
      if (Env.isConfigured) {
        await getIt<AuthService>().signOut();
        // Every feature Cubit is a get_it lazy singleton holding this
        // session's data in memory — reset them too, so a different
        // account signing in on the same device afterward (or just a
        // fresh sign-in) doesn't briefly see the previous owner's data
        // before anything happens to reload.
        await getIt.reset();
        setupServiceLocator();
      }
      if (context.mounted) {
        Navigator.of(context).pushNamedAndRemoveUntil(RouteNames.login, (route) => false);
      }
    },
  );
}

    required this.title,

    required this.fireAt,
    required this.read,
  });
